T-1.7.1.4 waits for the SaaS path, and T-1.7.1.7 is proposed.
**In progress 2026-11-05:** T-1.7.1.1 to T-1.7.1.3 shipped in PR [#415](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/415) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04), [D-141](decisions.md#d-141--a-connectors-search-raises-on-failure-a-refusal-is-a-24-hour-hold-on-a-sources-own-endpoint-non-video-sources-search-before-youtube-and-a-recording-counts-once-2026-11-05). T-1.7.1.4 waits for the SaaS path.
**Filed 2026-11-04** from PRs [#405](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/405) and [#408](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/408); proposed, not yet agreed. Since #408 Whisper is the main text path for podcasts, not a fallback.
- [x] T-1.7.1.1 🟢 `WHISPER_MAX_PER_JOB` counts episodes, not minutes, so one budget covers a 3-minute clip or a 76-minute show, and a split file counts once
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#415](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/415) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-141](decisions.md#d-141--a-connectors-search-raises-on-failure-a-refusal-is-a-24-hour-hold-on-a-sources-own-endpoint-non-video-sources-search-before-youtube-and-a-recording-counts-once-2026-11-05). `WHISPER_MAX_MINUTES_PER_JOB`, default 0 (no cap). It counts each recording's whole length, the larger of what the source declares and where the transcript ends, stops before the next transcription, and says so in the progress message and in `run_notes`. It also bounds subscription jobs. A recording that crosses the limit finishes. Whether to set it bears on OQ-34. #415 left the count limit, `WHISPER_MAX_PER_JOB`, off subscription jobs on purpose; #417 applied it the same day ([D-146](decisions.md#d-146--a-pool-never-carries-the-session-max_concurrent_jobs-is-per-user-a-key-check-says-good-or-bad-only-when-the-provider-did-and-whispers-key-and-count-hold-on-every-sync-2026-11-05), OQ-36).
- [x] T-1.7.1.2 🟢 `fetch_audio` holds a whole enclosure in memory while it is transcribed, up to about 150 MB an episode
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#415](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/415) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-141](decisions.md#d-141--a-connectors-search-raises-on-failure-a-refusal-is-a-24-hour-hold-on-a-sources-own-endpoint-non-video-sources-search-before-youtube-and-a-recording-counts-once-2026-11-05). `fetch_audio` is now `fetch_audio_to_file`, which streams to a temporary directory removed on every way out, chunks included. The 25 MB split path is unchanged. Streaming removes the memory limit that used to stop a runaway download, so there is a new 1 GB ceiling (`AUDIO_MAX_BYTES`, about 50 hours of speech).
- [x] T-1.7.1.3 🟢 Candidates are each show's newest 5 episodes whatever their subject. Taking shows in turn (#408) spreads the share across shows; it does not make an episode on topic
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#415](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/415) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-141](decisions.md#d-141--a-connectors-search-raises-on-failure-a-refusal-is-a-24-hour-hold-on-a-sources-own-endpoint-non-video-sources-search-before-youtube-and-a-recording-counts-once-2026-11-05). Each show offers the episodes that name most of the topic first, title matches ahead of description matches, then its newest. A show with no match contributes what it did. Feed data only, no LLM, no audio. Live: "The Nuclear Power Institute" went from 0 of 5 on topic to 5 of 5.
- [ ] T-1.7.1.4 ⚪ A podcast document keeps its feed URL in `documents.channel_id`, a `String(50)` foreign key to `channels`. SQLite enforces neither and Postgres would refuse it, so this belongs on the SaaS path
- [x] T-1.7.1.5 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-05, from #415)* A transcription that fails part-way is not charged to the minute cap, though its finished chunks were billed. `WHISPER_MAX_PER_JOB` has the same gap: the job cannot tell a failed Whisper call from a failed download, and D-111 keeps enforcement off the usage meter
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#424](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/424) (batch 7 of 2026-11-05). Linked decision: [D-149](decisions.md#d-149--fetch_text-may-raise-transcriptioncutshort-a-sync-reads-only-what-the-library-lacks-the-job-limit-never-refuses-a-subscribe-and-a-held-source-can-be-tried-anyway-2026-11-09). When a chunk after the first fails, `_transcribe_in_chunks` raises `source_failure.TranscriptionCutShort(seconds)`, which passes up through `fetch_text`. Both extraction loops leave the document unavailable and charge one recording to `WHISPER_MAX_PER_JOB` and its reach on the recording's timeline to the minute cap, so an overlap counts once, never the declared length and never from the usage meter. A failure on the first chunk billed nothing and still charges nothing. The exception carries a number and no provider text. Pinned down the podcast path and `YouTubeConnector` with the real `fetch_transcript`, through both tasks, with a fake OpenAI client and no request reaching a provider.
- [x] T-1.7.1.6 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-05, from #420)* `sources/podcast/connector.py` logs feed and audio URLs, and `PodcastClient.fetch_feed` still has httpx's request line, so a private feed's token can reach a worker log. T-3.7.2.9 covered the article path only
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#425](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/425) (batch 7 of 2026-11-05). Linked decision: [D-151](decisions.md#d-151--the-sockets-token-is-a-subprotocol-provider-errors-are-scrubbed-as-each-log-record-is-made-and-json-logs-hold-their-format-from-the-first-line-2026-11-09). Every line in `sources/podcast/connector.py` names the host and the exception's type, and `shared_url_fetch()` wraps `fetch_feed`, `fetch_audio_to_file` and the transcript fetch, so httpx's line is silenced there; the request still gets the whole URL. A feed entry with no guid goes by its link or audio file, so `loggable_id` takes a source id that holds a URL down to its host, in the connector, in the id handed to the Whisper helpers and on the `job_tasks` lines that name a candidate, #424's new line included. The other connectors and the Echo fetch paths print no fetched URL with a query. `PodcastConnector.search` keeps the httpx error as its cause, whose traceback quotes a feed URL; those feeds are public ones iTunes names.
- [x] T-1.7.1.7 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-09, from #424)* Whisper that answered but left no text, all chunks merged to nothing or a single file that came back empty, is billed and not charged to either limit. It is not a part-way failure and it is rare; `TranscriptionCutShort` would fit it
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#430](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/430) (batch 8 of 2026-11-09). Linked decision: [D-155](decisions.md#d-155--stored-exception-text-goes-through-error_summary-an-empty-whisper-answer-is-charged-a-refused-dispatch-fails-a-new-run-and-leaves-an-approval-waiting-and-a-start-the-queue-refused-uses-up-nothing-2026-11-09). `source_failure` gains a base class, `PaidTranscriptionLost(seconds)`. `TranscriptionCutShort` now derives from it, unchanged, and the new `TranscriptionEmpty` sits beside it rather than under it, since an empty answer is not a failure part-way. It is raised by YouTube's single-file path, by `_transcribe_in_chunks` when the merge is empty, and by the podcast single-file path. `seconds` is what Whisper reported hearing, never the declared length. `_fetch_text` catches the base class, so both extraction loops, the approved run and the subscription sync, charge one recording and those seconds, learning it from the connector and never from the meter ([D-111](decisions.md#d-111--operator-only-tier-switching-and-an-honest-pricing-page-2026-10-25)). Tested with silent Whisper fakes, single-file and split, for podcasts and for the real YouTube connector.
- [x] T-1.7.1.8 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-09, from #430)* The podcast single-file path drops a Whisper answer that has text but no `segments`, where YouTube builds pseudo-segments. Such an answer is now charged as empty, which is right for the limits, but its words are lost, as they were before #430
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#436](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/436) (batch 9 of 2026-11-09). Linked decision: [D-158](decisions.md#d-158--a-job-task-returns-at-once-on-a-failed-row-a-job-dates-an-undated-row-and-its-stored-chunks-whisper-text-without-segments-is-a-transcription-and-a-manual-dispatchs-503-promises-nothing-2026-11-09). In `sources/podcast/connector.py` a Whisper answer with text and no `segments` is kept as pseudo-segments through YouTube's own `_synthesize_pseudo_segments`, called on Whisper's reported duration rather than copied. The episode is read and charged once, as an ordinary transcription; an answer with no words at all is still `TranscriptionEmpty`. **Review fix:** the helper gives a segment only to a 30-second slot that gets a word, so sparse speech ended the timeline early, and with no length in the feed the minute cap charged less than Whisper billed. The new `_end_where_whisper_stopped` stretches the last pseudo-segment to the seconds Whisper reported. YouTube's helper is unchanged, since there the declared length covers the cap. Tested at the connector and through the extraction loop with a text-only Whisper fake.

### E-1.8 🟢 PDF / e-book connector

**Shipped:** 2026-05-03 — PR [#142](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/142). Closes Milestone **M-1.8** (PDF end-to-end).

**Linked decisions:** [D-034 — PDF source-type identity uses first-64KB SHA-256](decisions.md#d-034--pdf-source-type-identity-uses-first-64kb-sha-256-not-full-file-hash-2026-05-03), [D-035 — Connectors with no discovery surface raise `NotImplementedError`](decisions.md#d-035--connectors-with-no-discovery-surface-raise-notimplementederror-dispatcher-treats-as-zero-candidates-2026-05-03)

**Scope.** File upload (multipart) via `POST /api/v1/library/upload-pdf`. PyMuPDF text extraction; per-page boundaries preserved as segment metadata. Page-anchored citations via `permalink#page=<N>` deep-link fragments that standard PDF viewers (Chrome built-in, Firefox, Adobe) honour.

**Implementation notes.**
- First source type with no discovery surface — `search()` + `list_creator_items()` raise `NotImplementedError` per D-035; the dispatcher's `dispatch_search` already handles that gracefully per [D-026](decisions.md#d-026--sequential-fan-out-for-the-connector-dispatcher-2026-05-02).
- Identity: `source_id = f"pdf:{first_64kb_sha256}"` per D-034. Fast for very large PDFs; dedup-stable across trailer-metadata variation; idempotent re-upload.
- Raw bytes persist at `PDF_UPLOAD_DIR/<hash>.pdf` so future PRs can re-extract (e.g. when PyMuPDF improves table extraction) without re-uploading.
- `GET /api/v1/library/pdf/{digest}.pdf` serves bytes back so per-page deep-link citations work.
- Tables extracted via PyMuPDF's `find_tables()` and rendered inline with `[TABLE]` markers (future enhancement: emit as separate segments for better retrieval).
- 19 unit tests using real PyMuPDF (in-memory PDFs built via `fitz`'s writer; no fixture binaries).

**E-1.8 follow-ups.**
- [x] Frontend file-upload UI — *shipped 2026-10-19 PR [#224](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/224). Drop zone + file picker on the **Add sources** tab of `/library`; `.pdf` is enforced client-side before any byte leaves the browser. Files upload one at a time (the endpoint extracts and embeds synchronously, so parallel uploads only pile CPU on the API process) with a per-file progress bar, an "Extracting" phase once bytes are in, and a per-file result: Added / Already in library (`deduped=true`) / Failed with the 400 / 413 / 422 detail. The Sources list refreshes when the queue drains. Linked decision: [D-069](decisions.md#d-069--shipped-backend-surfaces-ingest-as-a-library-tab-author-studio-as-one-page-readiness-as-a-gate-mean-2026-10-19). Caveat resolved 2026-10-19: [S-5.10.4](#s-5104--key-document-visibility-on-document_id--shipped-2026-10-19) (PR [#241](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/241)) keyed visibility and the Chroma write on `document_id`, so an uploaded PDF is listed, searchable and retrievable by its owner and nobody else.*
- [x] Serving uploaded PDFs to a browser tab: `GET /library/pdf/{digest}.pdf` sits under the router-level `get_current_user` dependency, so a plain link or a citation's `#page=N` deep-link 401s from a new tab. The Library row fetches the bytes with the bearer header and opens a blob URL instead. *Shipped 2026-10-25 — PR [#380](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/380) (T-8.8.1.5), without the `?token=` pattern, which is retired: every PDF citation (Q&A chips, the reader's chips, card link and endnotes, Echo draft sources, the knowledge reader's "Open the source") fetches with the header and opens a blob URL with `#page=` kept, in a tab opened inside the click. Only the PDF route opens this way (`apiFiles.OPENABLE_API_FILES`), and the bytes are always served as `application/pdf`.*
- [x] Knowledge reports for non-video documents: `POST /videos/{id}/extract-knowledge` reads the transcript from `transcript_cache` by `video_id`, so an uploaded PDF or a pasted page gets a 422. Found 2026-10-25 while shipping S-5.10.5 (PR [#378](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/378)), which made their artifacts exportable; read the document's text by `document_id` instead.
  **Shipped:** 2026-10-29 — PR [#383](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/383) (batch 2 of 2026-10-25). Linked decision: [D-113](decisions.md#d-113--knowledge-text-for-any-source-the-transcript-cache-else-the-documents-chunks-put-back-together-2026-10-29). `document_text_service.full_text`, keyed on `document_id`, reads the `transcript_cache` row, else puts the document's chunks back together from the global collection: numeric `chunk_index` order, the `CHUNK_OVERLAP` window removed, one paragraph per chunk, and chunks left by an earlier, longer ingest dropped by chunk 0's `total_chunks` (a review fix). The Chroma read is fail-soft, so the router answers 422 only when no store holds text; visibility, the 409, the quota and the token budgets are unchanged. The knowledge prompts name the source by its type (`knowledge_prompts.SOURCE_PROFILES`), and a video's prompts are byte-identical. Every fetched Library row now has a Knowledge button for any source type, the job page opens the drawer by `document_id`, and the frames panel shows for videos only. Tests: `test_knowledge_non_video.py` (ingest through the real upload and paste routes, real chunking and an ephemeral Chroma), `test_document_text_service.py`, `test_knowledge_source_profiles.py`, `videoRowKnowledge.test.tsx`, `jobDetailKnowledge.test.tsx`, mutation-checked. Since #382, a document fetched by a topic run's search has a `transcript_cache` row keyed on `document_id`, so the job path no longer re-fetches it on reuse (the landing's side note) and its knowledge text comes from the cache. Follow-up: a length preference for knowledge reports stays [S-1.15.3](#s-1153--length-preference-for-knowledge-synthesis).
- OCR for image-only PDFs (Tesseract or cloud-OCR fallback gated like Playwright).
- Per-page Q&A reranking (chunk-level page filter on retrieval).

### E-1.9 🟢 Rename `channels` → `creators` (DB + ORM)

**Closed 2026-05-03 — PR [#146](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/146).** Closes I-1.

**Linked decision:** [D-032 — Operator-coordinated runbook for data-bearing identifier renames](decisions.md#d-032--operator-coordinated-runbook-vs-automatic-startup-migration-for-data-bearing-identifier-renames-2026-05-03)

**Scope.** Generalizes the YouTube-channel concept to any creator (podcast host, blog author, Twitter handle). Pure rename; no behavioral change. Two-phase per the D-032 precedent established by E-2.6:

- **Phase 1 (shipped, PR #146):** Python-level rename — `Creator` re-exported from new module `app.models.creator` as alias for `Channel`. Both names resolve to the same SQLAlchemy class. New code uses `Creator`; existing code using `Channel` keeps working. No DB changes.
- **Phase 2 (operator-coordinated runbook, deferred):** SQL-level rename — `channels` → `creators` table + `documents.channel_id` → `documents.creator_id` FK column via Alembic batch_alter_table. Documented in [`docs/migration-channels-to-creators.md`](migration-channels-to-creators.md). Self-hosters run the runbook on their own schedule; SaaS deployment runs it once for all tenants in a coordinated maintenance window when L5 ships.

**Why the same operator-coordinated split.** SQLite RENAME COLUMN with FK chain is non-atomic; default-keep-old-name matches operator expectations on `git pull master`; operators want side-by-side instances during evaluation. Same three reasons that drove D-032 originally.

**Note.** Plays the same role for creators as E-1.4 played for documents — and the same two-phase split E-2.6 used for brand identifier renames.

### E-1.10 🟢 Promote `video_id` PK to UUID `document_id`

**Promoted 2026-04-26** ahead of E-1.5 storage wiring per [D-015](decisions.md#d-015--promote-e-110-uuid-pk-ahead-of-reddit--hn-orchestrator-wiring-2026-04-26). Reddit (S-1.5.1) and HN (S-1.5.2) connectors are on master and emit namespaced `Candidate.source_id` strings (`reddit:<id>`, `hn:<id>`); landing E-1.10 first means their storage tasks (T-1.5.1.4, T-1.5.2.5) become trivial inserts on the new schema rather than a transitional namespaced-string PK that would need a second migration pass.

**Cadence: hard cutover** ([D-017](decisions.md#d-017--e-110-hard-cutover-single-migration-uuid-pk-promotion-2026-04-26), resolves OQ-7). Single Alembic migration, single PR — no transitional release. T-1.10.8 (round-trip migration test) is **gating** — the PR does not merge until both forward and rollback round-trip cleanly and the e2e smoke runs green on a real existing job. Every reader of `video_id` (14 importers across `youtube_service`, `chroma_service`, the five LangGraph agents, routers, and dataset exporters) gets visited and updated in the same PR; no `video_id` reads survive.

**Scheduling: four parallel tracks (2026-04-26).** Per user direction, four tracks proceed in parallel; each ships its own PR series and shares no files with the others:

- **Backend A — E-1.10** (this initiative): backend Alembic + ORM + FK retargeting.
- **Backend B — S-1.5.3**: `social_classify_stance` LLM use case + framing axis + threshold.
- **Frontend — T-1.5.4.1 / S-1.5.4**: polymorphic `<ApprovalCard>` primitive build.
- **I-2 remainder — E-2.5 (marketing site) and E-2.6 (code identifier rename)**: per the 2026-04-26 audit, E-2.1 / E-2.2 / E-2.3 / E-2.4 are already 🟢 (theme.ts + primitives + page migration + sidebar nav all shipped); the remaining I-2 work is the marketing landing page and the code-identifier cleanup with its data migration story.

Any can start first; none block the other. E-1.10 still gates Reddit / HN orchestrator wiring (D-015) and the e2e tests in S-1.5.11 (D-020); T-1.5.4.1 still gates Reddit / HN approval-UI rendering. S-1.5.11 (orchestrator dispatch) builds independently of E-1.10 but its e2e tests need E-1.10 + storage tasks to land.

**Scope.** Migrate `documents.video_id` PK column → `documents.document_id UUID` + a separate `documents.source_id text` column with `(source_type, source_id)` unique index. Cascade FK retargeting into `job_videos` (rename → `job_documents`) and `transcript_cache` (PK retargeted; rename → `text_cache` deferred unless trivial).

**Migration shape.**
- Add `document_id UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()` (Postgres) / `BLOB(16)` populated via Python (SQLite) — the project ships SQLite today.
- Backfill `document_id` for all ~912 existing rows.
- Add `source_id TEXT` populated from existing `video_id` (since `source_type='video'` everywhere today, the existing `video_id` *is* the YouTube ID — no namespace prefix).
- Drop `video_id` PK constraint; add `(source_type, source_id)` unique constraint.
- Retarget FK targets in `job_videos` → `job_documents.document_id`; in `transcript_cache` → `document_id`.
- Reversible: rollback drops the new columns, reinstates the legacy PK.

**Tasks**
- [x] T-1.10.1 Alembic migration adding `document_id` + `source_id` columns; backfill from `video_id`; populate UUIDs for all rows. *(shipped 2026-04-28 — PR [#96](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/96); `source_id` was already in place from L1 PR-1, so this PR adds `document_id VARCHAR(36)` only, backfills with UUID4 per row, alters to NOT NULL, creates a unique index. Doubles as a **merge node** joining the two parallel migration heads `01c5b6dae736` (rename) and `b8c9d0e1f2a3` (multi-source columns) that had been silently parallel since L1 PR-1. Drive-by fix: `alembic/env.py` stale `Video` import → `Document`)*
- [x] T-1.10.2 Drop legacy `video_id` PK + add `(source_type, source_id)` unique constraint *(landed in PR [#112](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/112) via explicit table-rebuild on documents — SQLite's anonymous PK constraint forced a manual CREATE NEW + INSERT FROM OLD + DROP + RENAME pattern rather than batch_alter_table)*
- [x] T-1.10.3 ORM updates (`Document.document_id` PK; relationships re-pointed) *(PR [#112](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/112). Document gets eager UUID4 factory at `__init__` time so callers reading `doc.document_id` pre-flush see a populated value)*
- [x] T-1.10.4 Rename `job_videos` → `job_documents` *(PR [#112](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/112). Table + ORM `__tablename__` updated; class name kept as JobVideo for back-compat with existing imports; before_insert event listener resolves document_id from video_id for legacy fixtures)*
- [x] T-1.10.5 Retarget `transcript_cache` PK to `document_id` *(PR [#112](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/112). Same before_insert resolver pattern as JobVideo)*
- [x] T-1.10.6 Chroma chunk metadata migration — *no-op since OQ-11 wiped Chroma; new chunks naturally key on document_id via the new ORM. Documented in the migration's docstring*
- [x] T-1.10.7 Update reading sites for the new PK shape *(PR [#112](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/112). Surgical updates: `db.get(Document, video_id)` patterns swapped for `db.query(Document).filter(Document.video_id == video_id).first()` in routers/knowledge.py and tasks/job_tasks.py. The 50+ readers that just print/log/return `video.video_id` for the YouTube native ID still work via the back-compat column — full elimination is an E-2.6 follow-on)*
- [x] T-1.10.8 Tests: round-trip migration + 415-test suite passes + e2e smoke *(PR [#112](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/112). `alembic upgrade head` + `alembic downgrade -1` cycle round-trips cleanly; full backend suite 415/415 green; FastAPI app imports cleanly with 42 routes)*

**Status: 🟢 shipped 2026-05-02** — PR [#112](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/112). All 8 sub-tasks closed.

**Unblocked downstream:** T-1.5.1.4 + T-1.5.2.5 (Reddit/HN storage — shipped 2026-05-02 PR [#113](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/113)). T-1.5.3.4 (classifier persistence — also PR #113). M-1.5 e2e tests (still pending orchestrator wiring).

### E-1.11 🟢 Transcript-pipeline resilience at scale

**Scope-changed 2026-10-29:** 🟡 → 🟢. [S-1.11.11](#s-11111--caption-track-choice-prefers-the-spoken-language-over-auto-dubs), the last open story, shipped in PR [#386](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/386) ([D-117](decisions.md#d-117--the-caption-track-is-the-one-in-the-spoken-language-auto-dubs-are-read-from-the-players-audio-tracks-2026-10-29)). Its owner step was done on 2026-11-03. What remains is a proposed task (T-1.11.11.4), two open questions for the owner (OQ-18, OQ-19) and S-1.11.7's deferred per-job Whisper control.

**Shipped:** 2026-07-22 — PR [#189](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/189). Recovery fully validated live on job 0d4db8c3 across three waves: 31/63 recovered 2026-07-22 (segmented Whisper + yt-dlp client ladder while the IP block was active), and the final 32 recovered 2026-07-24 after the block lifted (fast caption path, paced by the new limiter). **Final corpus: 200/200 videos — 162 youtube (591,843 words) + 38 whisper (355,396 words) = 947,239 transcript words.** The wait-out-the-block strategy from D-051 proved correct; cookies/proxy knobs (S-1.11.10) remain available for blocks that persist.

**Filed 2026-07-22** from the 200-video deep-research test (job `0d4db8c3`, 2026-07-21/22, ~96 min end-to-end, completed): **137/200 fetched, 63 lost** — 36 to the 25MB Whisper cap (disproportionately long-form conference talks / podcasts), 27 to yt-dlp HTTP 403s. A YouTube transcript-API IP block after ~60 serial fetches made Whisper the primary path for 80% of videos (159/200 attempts). All stories below ship together in this branch's PR.
**Linked decision:** [D-051](decisions.md#d-051--transcript-pipeline-resilience-bundle-circuit-breaker--segmented-whisper--yt-dlp-client-fallback-2026-07-22)

#### S-1.11.1 🟢 Transcript throttle + IP-block circuit breaker

**Scope.** Adaptive pacing + circuit breaker for transcript fetches. Config: `YOUTUBE_TRANSCRIPT_RATE_LIMIT` default raised 0.5s → 3.0s with ±40% jitter; breaker opens after 3 consecutive IP-block signals; cooldown 120s doubling to 900s max; post-cooldown probe re-tests before resuming. While open, a video falls to Whisper only when the remaining wait exceeds a cap — otherwise the loop waits it out.

#### S-1.11.2 🟢 Segmented Whisper for >25MB audio

**Scope.** ffmpeg stream-copy chunking into ~20MB-target time chunks with 15s overlap; per-chunk transcription; merge with timestamps offset by each chunk's position; overlap-zone segments deduplicated by midpoint-ownership rule. `worstaudio` (smallest-audio-format) re-download fallback when ffmpeg is unavailable; only bails if still oversize. Design per [D-051](decisions.md#d-051--transcript-pipeline-resilience-bundle-circuit-breaker--segmented-whisper--yt-dlp-client-fallback-2026-07-22) part (b).

#### S-1.11.3 🟢 yt-dlp 403 hardening

**Scope.** Escalating retry ladder across YouTube innertube player clients (default → android → ios) with cooldowns between attempts; smaller-audio-format preference.

#### S-1.11.4 🟢 Transcript provenance fix

**Scope.** `fetch_transcript` returns its source; `transcript_cache` gains a `source` column via Alembic; `documents.transcript_source` is now honest — was hardcoded `'youtube'` (the 200-video run recorded 0 of ~96 Whisper transcripts as `'whisper'`).

#### S-1.11.5 🟢 Paginated YouTube search

**Scope.** `pageToken` loop in `search_videos`; `YOUTUBE_SEARCH_MAX_PAGES` default 2. Quota note: each page costs 100 units.

#### S-1.11.6 🟢 Unresolved-handle surfacing

**Scope.** Topic jobs store `{"resolved": [], "unresolved": []}` in `channel_list_resolved`; approval UI warns when channel handles failed to resolve (previously each failed handle silently burned a 100-unit search fallback with no user-facing signal).

#### S-1.11.7 🟢 Per-job Whisper budget

**Scope.** `WHISPER_MAX_PER_JOB` env var, default 50; videos beyond the budget are marked unavailable with a reason. Per-job UI control ⚪ deferred follow-up.

#### S-1.11.8 🟢 Extraction ETA + search-phase sub-progress

**Scope.** Extraction ETA in progress messages driven by rolling per-path pacing (transcript-API vs Whisper rates differ ~10×); search phase emits sub-progress stages (was a static 5% for the whole ~3.7 min phase).

#### S-1.11.9 🟢 Recovery tooling

**Scope.** `scripts/retry_unavailable_transcripts.py` re-fetches a job's unavailable documents, then chunks + embeds them into the global collection. Validated on job `0d4db8c3`'s 63 lost videos.

#### S-1.11.11 🟢 Caption-track choice prefers the spoken language over auto-dubs
**PR:** [#386](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/386)
**Shipped:** 2026-10-29 — PR [#386](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/386) (batch 2 of 2026-10-25). Measured first ([note](notes/2026-10-25-caption-track-choice.md): 37 Hindi, Hinglish and Kannada library videos, 1 quota unit; the old and new rules differ on 7 of 35). `fetch_transcript` takes the caption track in the spoken language and never an auto-dub: dubs are read from the player response's audio tracks (`<lang>.4` original, `<lang>.10` dub) and an automatic track whose code exactly equals a dub's is dropped; the spoken language is the first of `defaultAudioLanguage` (looked up lazily, at most once per fetch), the original audio track, or a single-audio video's ASR language that some non-dub track is in; manual beats automatic; a list of nothing but dubs goes to Whisper, never ingested. The chosen track is stored on `documents.source_metadata_json["caption_track"]` (no migration). New scripts: `scripts/refetch_dubbed_transcripts.py` (dry run by default) and `scripts/measure_caption_tracks.py` (read-only). Tests: `test_caption_track_choice.py` (real `youtube-transcript-api` parsing, only HTTP faked), `test_caption_track_at_caller.py` (`execute_subscription_job`, `resume_job_after_approval`, `retry_unavailable_transcripts`), `test_refetch_dubbed_transcripts.py`, mutation-checked. **Owner step done 2026-11-03:** `refetch_dubbed_transcripts.py --write` re-fetched the three dubbed library transcripts.
**Linked decision:** [D-117](decisions.md#d-117--the-caption-track-is-the-one-in-the-spoken-language-auto-dubs-are-read-from-the-players-audio-tracks-2026-10-29)
**Proposed for its own session 2026-10-19** (from S-1.16.2), with a brief covering T-1.11.11.1–3 and a measurement step first (list caption tracks and `defaultAudioLanguage` for the ~30 Hindi/Hinglish videos in the dev library).

**Raised in priority 2026-10-19 by the S-1.16.2 held-out evaluation: 2 of 13 Hindi-speech videos in that study arrived as machine-dubbed tracks (one Bengali, one English: `v6CuYULP3tM` lists `en-US(auto)` and `hi(auto)`), and an English dub is undetectable downstream — every quote from it is words the speaker never said.** **Filed 2026-10-19** from the S-1.16.2 corpus build ([D-080](decisions.md#d-080--code-mixing-detection-a-lexicon-tagger-not-an-llm-pass-2026-10-19)). `_fetch_transcript_once` tries `en` / `en-auto` and then takes the FIRST listed track. Video `1v3_AQ26jZ0` (a Hindi lecture) lists `bn(auto)`, `en-US(auto)`, `hi(auto)`: `en-US` matched nothing, so the library now holds a machine-dubbed Bengali transcript of a Hindi lecture.
- [x] T-1.11.11.1 Match regional English codes (`en-US`, `en-GB`) as English, but only when the track is the video's original audio language — *shipped 2026-10-29 (#386), by a different rule: a dub is recognised by its exact code, and every other track is matched to the spoken language on its primary subtag, so a regional English track counts as English once the dubs are gone. Matching dubs on the language would have thrown away `IcVYyF3EEbk`'s only real track*
- [x] T-1.11.11.2 When English is unavailable, prefer the original-audio track over auto-dubbed ones; take the default audio language from the Data API (`snippet.defaultAudioLanguage`) when present — *shipped 2026-10-29 (#386). The spoken language comes first, English-first only when nothing names it. `defaultAudioLanguage` is the first of three signals, looked up only when the non-dub tracks span more than one language (2 of 35 measured); the player's original audio track is the second, because `v6CuYULP3tM` declares nothing (OQ-18)*
- [x] T-1.11.11.3 Re-fetch documents whose stored transcript language differs from their default audio language (one-off script, reporting counts) — *shipped 2026-10-29 (#386) as `scripts/refetch_dubbed_transcripts.py`: it lists the tracks of every cached candidate (a declaration is no proof), paced per D-051, and stops at the first IP-block signal. The library dry run listed 93 of 93 candidates and found 3 to re-fetch: `1v3_AQ26jZ0` (bn → hi), `5AuuOZ4vuxI` (bn → hi), `v6CuYULP3tM` (en-US → hi). **Owner step done 2026-11-03:** run with `--write` the same day the services were restarted on master; it rewrote the three transcript cache rows, their documents and their chunks*
- [x] T-1.11.11.4 🟢 *(proposed 2026-10-29, from #386's review)* Carry `get_video_details`' `snippet.defaultAudioLanguage` onto the document and into `fetch_transcript(audio_language=...)`, so the lazy lookup is never needed. Deferred because it touches `job_tasks.py`, the connector metadata and the document upsert, which the parallel batch was editing. The lookup costs at most 1 unit per newly fetched video whose non-dub tracks span several languages, and nothing on a cache hit; worth doing if the quota log shows it
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#418](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/418) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-144](decisions.md#d-144--a-fifth-voice-label-other-names-no-language-to-write-in-a-job-fills-gaps-and-only-the-backfill-rewrites-and-the-declared-audio-language-is-a-key-on-the-document-2026-11-05). `get_video_details` returns `defaultAudioLanguage`, free in the `snippet` part it already pays for, and it travels through the connector metadata and the search agent into the document upsert, then into `fetch_transcript(audio_language=...)`. It is stored at `source_metadata_json["default_audio_language"]`, with no migration. The key holding null means the video declares nothing, which also spares the lookup; no key means nobody looked, so existing documents keep today's lookup. **It does not pay for itself in quota:** by timing the lookup has cost 0 units, with a hard ceiling of 5, and no real job has run it yet. The gain is that a fetch stops depending on the Data API.
- [x] T-1.11.11.5 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-05, from #418)* `api_quota_log` records the lazy lookup as `videos`, the same as `get_video_details`, so the log cannot name it. Not needed for #418, but the next quota question hits the same wall
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#424](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/424) (batch 7 of 2026-11-05). Linked decision: [D-149](decisions.md#d-149--fetch_text-may-raise-transcriptioncutshort-a-sync-reads-only-what-the-library-lacks-the-job-limit-never-refuses-a-subscribe-and-a-held-source-can-be-tried-anyway-2026-11-09). `get_default_audio_languages` logs as `videos_audio_language`, listed in `OPERATION_COSTS` at 1 unit and mapped to `video_metadata` in `YOUTUBE_FEATURES`, so `/health/quota` and every tenant's figures are unchanged. `refetch_dubbed_transcripts.py` shares the function, so it carries the label too. Older rows keep `videos`.
- [x] T-1.11.11.6 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-05, from #418, which found it reading the quota log)* `execute_subscription_job`'s `db.get(Document, vid)` compares a YouTube id with the UUID primary key, so every unlinked video counts as missing and its details are re-read, at 1 unit per 50 videos. Harmless here
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#424](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/424) (batch 7 of 2026-11-05). Linked decision: [D-149](decisions.md#d-149--fetch_text-may-raise-transcriptioncutshort-a-sync-reads-only-what-the-library-lacks-the-job-limit-never-refuses-a-subscribe-and-a-held-source-can-be-tried-anyway-2026-11-09). `_videos_in_library` looks videos up by `Document.video_id`, in batches of 500, so a sync reads details only for what the library lacks, and a library hit is linked and granted as before ([D-063](decisions.md#d-063--per-user-isolation-shared-cache-private-catalogue-2026-07-31)). A row now carries only what the details said: the loop's `"Unknown"` default would have renamed a document another tenant ingested, for everyone. A placeholder row titled "Unknown" counts as not yet read, so a video that goes public is corrected by the next sync. A grep found no other site with this mistake. A sync no longer refreshes a read document's title or thumbnail, which the bug did by accident.

**Open questions**
- OQ-18 (from S-1.11.11): the brief said to take `defaultAudioLanguage` from the Data API for every video. The build looks it up only when the tracks offer a choice, passes over a declaration that no track is in, and otherwise uses the player's original audio track, because `v6CuYULP3tM`, one of the two known dubs, declares nothing ([D-117](decisions.md#d-117--the-caption-track-is-the-one-in-the-spoken-language-auto-dubs-are-read-from-the-players-audio-tracks-2026-10-29), first alternative). Does the owner accept this order? The owner's call; nothing changes until it is ruled.
- OQ-19 (from S-1.11.11): `9tigHWH-UD8` declares `en`, so its manual English track is ingested although YouTube's recogniser heard Hindi. On Hinglish speech both readings are plausible, and no free signal settles it. Keep the declaration first while a track is in it, or let the recogniser's language win when the two disagree? The owner's call; nothing changes until it is ruled.

### E-1.12 🟢 LLM context-window resilience at scale

**Filed 2026-07-29** from the context-scale audit (five parallel readers + adversarial verification over every agentic call site, sized against a 200-video / 10.6M-token extreme corpus; reference job 0d4db8c3). Findings summarized in [architecture.md § Context-window & scale behavior](architecture.md#context-window--scale-behavior-audited-2026-07-29). **Shipped 2026-07-29** — PRs [#195](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/195) + [#196](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/196) (all 9 stories) plus the D-052 model re-audit: context windows measured empirically (nano/mini 272K, 5.4/5.5/5.6 922K, 4.1-mini 1,047,576), three-tier model stack restored (nano / 5.4-mini / 5.5), four use cases stepped down a price tier. Report batches now derive from the resolved model with a 120K quality cap; reduce recurses to a single summary; map failures bisect-retry then surface as a processing note in the report itself.

**Why it exists.** The report pipeline sizes batches to one global constant (`LLM_MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS` = 1,047,576) blind to the resolved model; reduce is single-level; failed batches and channel-path overflow are silently dropped; `rank_and_curate` sends the whole pool in one prompt (~300K tokens at the new target=500 cap). Q&A surfaces are corpus-invariant (top-K bounded) and need no work.

**Stories**
- S-1.12.1 🟢 **Fix job-scoped Q&A retrieval** — qa_agent.py:111 uses the deprecated `query_collection` signature; job filter ignored → every job Q&A searches the whole global library (CLAUDE.md's scoping claim currently false). One-line fix + regression test. *Correctness bug, not scale.*
- S-1.12.2 🟢 Per-model `context_window` in `USE_CASE_REGISTRY`; derive report/channel batch budgets from the resolved model with a safety margin (replaces the global constant).
- S-1.12.3 🟢 Recursive reduce: loop pairwise until under budget + final merge; on reduce failure degrade to truncated summaries, never raw pass-through to compose.
- S-1.12.4 🟢 Loud accounting for dropped content: failed map batches, channel-path truncation, knowledge 60K tail-chop → progress message + report footnote ("N sources partially processed").
- S-1.12.5 🟢 Batch/tournament ranking in `rank_and_curate`; cap map batches ~120K tokens for extraction quality (context-rot mitigation independent of window fit).
- S-1.12.6 🟢 Catch context-length 400s → bisect batch and retry (adaptive splitting).
- S-1.12.7 🟢 Wire `max_tokens` from registry `typ_out` at all 20 call sites (4 set it today).
- S-1.12.8 🟢 Knowledge agent: windowed extraction above 60K instead of silent truncation; synthesize from extraction JSON without re-sending the full transcript.
- S-1.12.9 🟢 Doc drift: CLAUDE.md says CHUNK_SIZE 512/50 but config ships 256/32; registry holds 20 use cases, docs say 19.

Linked decision: [D-054](decisions.md#d-054--per-user-llm-overrides--cost-calculator-gpt-56-adaptive-reasoning-findings-2026-07-29).

**Scope.** Expose all 20 per-use-case LLM settings (provider/model/reasoning) in user settings with DB-backed overrides that dynamically supersede registry defaults (ContextVar layer inside `byok_context` per D-041), plus a cost calculator benchmarked on the real 200-video job with researched cross-provider pricing (OpenAI / Anthropic / Google, source-cited, `as_of` stamped).

- S-1.13.6 ⚪ Responses-API path for guaranteed `max` reasoning on `search_rank_and_curate` (follow-up)

See D-054 amendments I + II.

### E-1.14 🟡 Model-tier quality evaluation harness

**Filed 2026-07-29.** Linked decision: [D-055](decisions.md#d-055--intelligence-delta-evaluation-methodology-production-input-replay--blind-ab-judging-2026-07-29). Realizes invention [N-001](inventions.md#n-001--production-replay-blind-delta-harness-for-per-call-site-model-tier-selection-2026-07-29). Docs PR: [#201](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/201).

**Why it exists.** Every model-tier decision through D-052/D-053/D-054 rested on proxy evidence — latency, context windows, JSON discipline, task-shape reasoning. None of it measured the thing that matters: **how much answer quality the cheap tiers actually give up on our corpus.** E-1.14 answers that empirically, per use case, and the verdicts feed back into `USE_CASE_REGISTRY` defaults and the E-1.13 override guidance.

**Method** (D-055): rebuild a completed job's exact per-call-site inputs from stored state using the app's own deterministic transforms, replay them through frontier models at maximum thinking, blind A/B judge against what production stored, with judges verifying claims against the live corpus rather than scoring prose.

**Reference run.** Job `0d4db8c3` — 200 videos, 1,057,331 transcript words, 5,149 chunks, 12 map batches at ≤120K tokens. *Corrected 2026-10-25 ([S-1.14.21](#e-114--model-tier-quality-evaluation-harness)): 1,057,331 counted every chunk overlap twice; the transcripts hold 947,239 words.*

**Stories**
- S-1.14.1 🟢 Deterministic input-replay prep — rebuild chunks/batches/stats/retrieval from stored job state via the app's own chunker + batch budgeting; dump stored artifacts (report, Q&A, knowledge) for pairing. *Verified: reproduced 5,149 chunks / 12 batches.*
- S-1.14.2 🟢 Challenger replication at max effort across the pipeline — map ×12 (5,861 items, 200/200 videos), reduce, compose, full Q&A chain ×2, knowledge chain ×2, query planning. *Mixed provenance: Q&A + knowledge on Fable 5, report chain on Opus 5 after a subscription-limit switch — labelled per artifact, not averaged.*
- S-1.14.3 🟢 Blind pairing + rubric judging with retrieval-grounded verification. **Result: max-effort won all 6 comparisons** — report severe, knowledge ×2 / Q&A-context / query-plan moderate, Q&A-patterns minor. Scores in [D-055 Results](decisions.md#d-055--intelligence-delta-evaluation-methodology-production-input-replay--blind-ab-judging-2026-07-29).
- S-1.14.4 🟢 Loss attribution for unpersisted stages. **Result: 60 of 60 sampled facts from two batches absent from the shipped report (100% loss).** Root cause is the map/reduce token funnel, not model IQ — see S-1.14.8.
- S-1.14.5 🟢 Selection-criteria audit standing in for `search_rank_and_curate` (true re-rank impossible — rejected pool not persisted). *Finding: default selection graded 6.5/10 — ~20-24 questionable picks, IBM Technology at 10% of corpus despite not being a preferred channel, 12 of 30 preferred channels absent, zero flagship model-release coverage despite instructions naming GPT-5.x / Claude / Gemini.*
- **S-1.14.6 🟢 Persist agent intermediates + rejected candidate pool — shipped 2026-10-19** so quality is continuously monitorable instead of replay-only — see [architecture.md § Agent-intermediate retention gap](architecture.md#agent-intermediate-retention-gap-identified-2026-07-29). **Candidate-pool half shipped 2026-07-30**, PR [#205](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/205): `job_search_candidates` stores the whole pool with a `selected` flag. **Map/reduce half shipped 2026-10-19**, PR [#273](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/273), linked decision [D-088](decisions.md#d-088--report-intermediates-persisted-per-run-on-by-default-frozen-at-the-stage-that-produced-them-2026-10-19).
  - [x] T-1.14.6.1 `job_report_intermediates` (migration `a3b4c5d6e7f9`): one row per map call — bisected halves and failed batches included, with error and covered videos — plus one reduce row with its processing notes, grouped by `run_id`
  - [x] T-1.14.6.2 Captured through `run_report_agent(..., intermediates_out=)` (the search agent's `candidates_out` pattern); payloads serialised when each stage produces them, so compose's later item mutations never reach the record
  - [x] T-1.14.6.3 Persisted best-effort by the job task before the HTML is built, behind `REPORT_INTERMEDIATES_ENABLED` (default on, ~3 MB per 200-video run); `scripts/regenerate_report.py --persist-intermediates` adds a run next to the original
  - [x] T-1.14.6.4 `scripts/audit_report_intermediates.py` reads a run back: map calls by status, items by category, dropped chunks, and the reduce identity (in − duplicates − trimmed = out)
  - [x] T-1.14.6.5 Tests (`test_report_intermediates.py`): rows written and lossless on the real graph with fake LLMs, failed / unparsed batches recorded as rows, records frozen before compose mutates items, one `run_id` per run, setting-off path writes nothing, best-effort on a failing session, channel jobs write nothing, the job task's wiring, and the audit script
  *Status reconciled 2026-10-18; it had been left at ⚪.*
- **S-1.14.7 🟢 Measured verdicts as registry guidance, shown in `/account/ai-models` — shipped 2026-10-19**, PR [#251](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/251). The defaults half was already done decision by decision (D-057, D-062, D-073); this story moved the evidence behind them out of `decisions.md` and in front of the person choosing an override. No `default_config` changed. Evidence folded in: [D-052](decisions.md#d-052--measured-context-windows--three-tier-model-stack-e-112-model-re-audit-2026-07-29), [D-055](decisions.md#d-055--intelligence-delta-evaluation-methodology-production-input-replay--blind-ab-judging-2026-07-29), [D-056](decisions.md#d-056--report-pipeline-budgets-derived-from-work-lossless-reduce-sectioned-compose-2026-07-30), [D-057](decisions.md#d-057--volume-tier-moves-to-gpt-56-luna-selection-is-now-measurable-2026-07-30), [D-060](decisions.md#d-060--extraction-ratio-raised-020---040-2026-07-30), [D-061](decisions.md#d-061--s-11410-re-judge-the-intelligence-gap-after-the-fixes-2026-07-31), [D-062](decisions.md#d-062--report_compose-upgraded-to-claude-opus-5-2026-07-31), [D-072](decisions.md#d-072--two-dates-per-source-era-at-ingest-time-as-a-ranking-preference-qa-statistics-from-the-database-2026-10-19), [D-073](decisions.md#d-073--frame-description-moves-to-gemini-31-flash-lite-on-image-grounded-grading-selection-stays-on-gpt-55-2026-10-19).
  - [x] T-1.14.7.1 `UseCaseInfo.guidance` (required): `measured` entries carry decision id, date, the gap to the flagship tier, what fails on a downgrade and the measured cost; `unmeasured` entries carry a note on what evidence exists. **8 measured** (`qa_formulate_answer`, `knowledge_synthesize_report`, `search_plan_queries`, `report_map_chunks`, `report_reduce_summaries`, `report_compose`, `visual_select_moments`, `visual_describe_frame`), **15 unmeasured**
  - [x] T-1.14.7.2 `ModelVerdict` per use case, scoped to reasoning levels: unacceptable — luna for frame description (22/66 unusable), nano for map (unparseable JSON on 1 of 2 batches), luna with reasoning on for map and era extraction, gpt-5.4-mini with reasoning on for the three mini sites; weaker — sonnet-5 for compose, mini and flash-lite for frame selection
  - [x] T-1.14.7.3 `assess_choice()` (verdicts + `VISION_CAPABLE_MODELS`); `/health/llm` reports guidance and warnings for the resolved config; `GET /settings/llm` ships guidance, a `vision` flag and `vision_capable_models`; the estimate prices each row on the default too
  - [x] T-1.14.7.4 Page: evidence badge, warning callouts, warning markers on model options before one is picked, benchmark cost against the default, collapsible evidence panel. Verified live on a scratch stack: compose on sonnet-5 shows `$3.03 against $7.58 (−$4.54)`, total $7.65 vs $12.20, matching D-062
  - [x] T-1.14.7.5 Tests: every use case measured-with-decision or unmeasured; every cited decision exists in `decisions.md`; verdict models are real ids; no default trips a warning; endpoint and `/health/llm` tests
  - [x] T-1.14.7.6 In passing: `qa_history_*` rows were grouped under "Job Q&A" (prefix order); two tests still iterated probe targets as bare names after #240
  - **Design choices made here, open to revision** (recorded as [D-076](decisions.md#d-076--model-guidance-measured-means-the-use-cases-own-output-was-graded-verdicts-warn-never-block-2026-10-19), not decided by the user):
    1. `measured` means this use case's own output was graded. The refine, extract and library-answer steps sat inside graded chains or moved tier alongside graded ones, and are `unmeasured` with a note rather than borrowing a sibling's verdict.
    2. "Unacceptable" on n=2 for nano on map batches (D-057 itself acted on n=2). "Weaker" for frame selection rests on agreement with the default's picks, which D-073 says is not quality; the copy says so.
    3. Warnings advise and never block a save, matching the fail-soft rule for the vision allowlist ([D-067](decisions.md#d-067--visual-understanding-separate-frames-table-marker-in-text-annotation-opt-in-per-job-2026-07-31)). A hard block for `unacceptable` would be one line in the PUT handler — see OQ-16.
- **S-1.14.8 🟢 Fix the report information funnel — shipped 2026-07-30.** Linked decision: [D-056](decisions.md#d-056--report-pipeline-budgets-derived-from-work-lossless-reduce-sectioned-compose-2026-07-30). Validated on job `0d4db8c3`: reduce now loses **0.00%** (5,861 items in / out, 200 videos + 92 channels retained, vs 91.2% of items and 46% of videos lost before).
  - [x] T-1.14.8.1 `report_map_chunks` completion budget derived from batch size (3,000 → ~24,000); registry `p95_input` corrected 32,000 → 120,000; new measured `MODEL_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` (128K / 64K haiku)
  - [x] T-1.14.8.2 Reduce rewritten deterministic + lossless; even per-video compression past a 1M-token cost guard so no video is dropped wholesale
  - [x] T-1.14.8.3 Compose sectioned — one call per section, split further as material requires, so report length scales with corpus; Statistics rendered in code
  - [x] T-1.14.8.4 Reduce trims / failed sections / map drops disclosed as an in-report processing note
  - [x] T-1.14.8.5 Re-validated end to end (see D-056 Validation); isolating residual *model* delta needs a fresh full-corpus judging run — tracked as S-1.14.10

- **S-1.14.9 🟢 Two corpus traps — shipped 2026-07-30.** (a) Every extracted item carried an empty `video_url` because the map chunk header never supplied one, so **every timestamp citation in every report was a dead link** (`href="&t=123"`); the header now carries the URL and composed HTML unwraps any non-URL anchor. Validated: 104/104 links working, 6/6 videos correct, 0 fabricated. (b) `_item_video()` keys on `video_url` rather than title, so the two pairs of distinct videos whose titles differ only in letter case no longer merge.
- **S-1.14.10 🟢 E-1.14 harness re-run after the fixes — closed 2026-07-31.** Linked decision: [D-061](decisions.md#d-061--s-11410-re-judge-the-intelligence-gap-after-the-fixes-2026-07-31). Three independent blind judges on the regenerated production report vs the max-effort control, same corpus: **gap SEVERE → MODERATE**, mean **3.5 → 6.94** against the control's 8.72. Zero fabrications in either report. Production now exceeds the control on words (30,864 vs 26,631) and citations (1,489 vs 946); trails on video coverage (185 vs 200) and quantitative density.
- **S-1.14.14 🟢 Residual compose defects closed — shipped 2026-07-31** — PR [#209](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/209). Linked decision: [D-062](decisions.md#d-062--report_compose-upgraded-to-claude-opus-5-2026-07-31).
  - [x] T-1.14.14.1 Source in the VISIBLE citation text. Measured: citations with a source named nearby **30% → 63%**; attribution score **6 → 9**, now ahead of the control's 7
  - [x] T-1.14.14.2 Theme-first structure. Measured: sub-sections named after a channel **16% → 7%**; corpus titles mentioned **3 → 139** of 200. Did NOT move the insight score
  - [x] T-1.14.14.3 `_invoke_with_retry` with an error classifier — 7 sections, 0 failed, where a `529` had cost one
  - [x] T-1.14.14.4 Statistics marked computed-not-estimated — **partially effective**; residual defects carried to S-1.14.15
  - [x] T-1.14.14.5 Re-judged; overall **6.94 → 7.22**, coverage gap moderate → minor, synthesis unchanged at 6.33 → tier upgrade justified per the pre-registered rule
- **S-1.14.16 🟢 `report_compose` → claude-opus-5 — shipped 2026-07-31.** [D-062](decisions.md#d-062--report_compose-upgraded-to-claude-opus-5-2026-07-31). Head-to-head on identical input: synthesis 9 vs 6-7, tension-handling 9 vs 6, specificity 9-10 vs 5.5-6. The length prior was tested and refuted — opus is 2.6x longer AND ~1.4x denser (3.42 vs 2.42 claims/100w), so ~3.5x payload. sonnet dropped ~8 corpus debates, named 18 tools vs 47, contradicted itself on a consensus claim, and invented two host names. Cost $7.65 → $12.20 per benchmark job.
- **S-1.14.15 🟢 Executive-summary factual defects (T-1.14.14.4 residue) — shipped 2026-10-19**, PR [#227](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/227), batched with S-1.17.2 (same prompt files). Linked decision: [D-072](decisions.md#d-072--two-dates-per-source-era-at-ingest-time-as-a-ranking-preference-qa-statistics-from-the-database-2026-10-19).
  - [x] T-1.14.15.1 Summary mis-dates the corpus — the statistics arrived as a raw JSON dump whose 92-entry channel breakdown buried the dates. Every compose call now receives a compact `_corpus_facts` block (count, words, publication span, per-year histogram, discussed-era span) and is told to quote it exactly
  - [x] T-1.14.15.2 Pipeline metadata leaks into prose — the summary only ever received section titles with item counts ("Key Facts, part 1 of 2: 1,128 items"), so counts were all it could summarise. It now reads a plain-text rendering of the written sections (`_section_text_digest`, 60K-token bound) and is told never to mention counts, parts or batches
  - [x] T-1.14.15.3 "well over 150 distinct videos" — the same verified facts block goes to every section prompt, not just the summary; pinned by a test that captures every compose prompt
  - [x] T-1.14.15.4 Judging harness — `backend/scripts/judge_report_sections.py` splits a report on `<h2>` and judges each section in full; no document-level cap remains
- **S-1.14.11 🟢 Reasoning-model response shape — shipped 2026-07-30.** Anthropic returns `[thinking_block, text_block]` instead of a string once the model actually thinks, so `response.content` yielded a list exactly on the hardest prompts. Latent in production since D-053 moved these sites to claude-sonnet-5. Fixed centrally via `llm_service.response_text()` at all 18 response-reading call sites, with regression tests.

- S-1.14.17 🟢 A composed section can end mid-tag. Found 2026-10-19 by S-8.8.1's parser fixtures: the stored report for job `419e691d…` (the R1 measurement run) has a `<a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5` cut off where one section ends and the next begins, so every HTML parser folds the next section's `<h2>` and `<h3>` into that attribute value and two headings vanish from the outline, the iframe and the reader alike. Likely a completion-budget cut in `report_compose`; the compose step should detect an unbalanced tail (unterminated tag or attribute) and either retry the section or close it cleanly, and disclose it in the processing note like the other drops. Not a reader concern — the reader refuses to link the garbage href and nothing more can be recovered client-side.
  **Shipped:** 2026-10-25 — PR [#379](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/379), with the reader half (T-8.8.4.4). Already-stored reports are not rewritten: the reader can show their processing note but cannot recover the headings they lost.
  - [x] T-1.14.17.1 Detect: `app/utils/html_balance.py` `check()` scans a fragment once and reports a tail inside a tag, an attribute value (quoted or unquoted) or a comment, and the elements left open. Void and self-closing tags open nothing, optional end tags close where a parser closes them, stray end tags are ignored, and a `/` counts as self-closing only outside every attribute value (`<a href=/x/>` stays open)
  - [x] T-1.14.17.2 Retry, then close and disclose: `report_agent._compose_checked` wraps every topic-report section and the executive summary. An unfinished answer is composed once more at twice the completion budget, clamped to the model's ceiling. If the retry is unfinished too, `html_balance.repair()` cuts the longer attempt before the broken tag and closes its open elements, and the processing note names the section (titles escaped). A retry that fails or comes back empty keeps the first attempt, closed, with its own wording ("could not be composed again"). `processing_notes` gains `report_sections_retried` and `report_sections_repaired`, and the note paragraph carries `class="processing-note"`
  - [x] T-1.14.17.3 Channel narratives go through the same check (`compose_channel_report`, use case `report_compose_channel_section`) and append the same classed note when one has to be closed
  - [x] T-1.14.17.4 Tests: `test_html_balance.py`, `test_report_section_balance.py` (mocked model; down to `run_report_agent` for topic and channel jobs) and `test_report_body.py` (a report cut twice, saved and served through `/jobs/{id}/report/body` with the note intact). Mutation-checked: skipping the check fails 7 of 8 section tests, skipping the repair 4, dropping the disclosure 5

- S-1.14.21 🟢 The corpus word count counted chunk overlap twice. Found 2026-10-25 while fixing S-1.14.17. `compute_statistics` summed each chunk's `word_count`, so every `CHUNK_OVERLAP` region was counted twice, and on a visual run the `[VISUAL @ ...]` descriptions were counted as words: the 200-video reference job reported 1,057,331 words for 947,239 in its transcripts, and R1 run 10 reported 55,443 for 42,999.
  **Shipped:** 2026-10-25 — PR [#379](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/379).
  - [x] T-1.14.21.1 Count each document once. `chunk_transcript` stamps every chunk with `document_word_count` (the document's full text with `[VISUAL @ ...]` spans removed); `compute_statistics` counts each document once from that stamp, and a channel's words are the sum of its documents'. Chunks from before the stamp are put back together in `chunk_index` order without their overlap (`quote_fidelity.merge_chunk_texts`), annotations stripped, and counted. Tests on real `chunk_transcript` output for overlap alone, overlap plus visual spans, and unstamped chunks rebuilt from a shuffled order, with per-channel breakdowns and at `run_report_agent`; master's code fails all of them (74 against 64 for overlap alone, 132 against 64 with visual spans)

- S-1.14.18 🔵 Refresh the guidance that S-1.14.7 shows as stale or unmeasured. Filed 2026-10-19. Linked decision: [D-076](decisions.md#d-076--model-guidance-measured-means-the-use-cases-own-output-was-graded-verdicts-warn-never-block-2026-10-19). Several `measured` entries show users numbers taken on a tier the slot no longer runs, and some of the busiest slots are unmeasured. Each task below is a D-055-style replay on the reference job. A changed verdict is a registry edit plus a decision entry. **Accepted 2026-10-25** ([D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25)): the comparison's pass rule (T-1.14.20.4) is pre-registered before it runs; S-1.14.18 and S-1.17.4 are scheduled with it because all three spend the shared Gemini 3.1 Pro judge quota. T-1.14.18.6 is deferred with R1 ([D-109](decisions.md#d-109--r1-is-parked-visual-analysis-stays-hidden-its-measurement-stops-the-code-stays-2026-10-25)).
  - [ ] T-1.14.18.1 `qa_formulate_answer` at medium reasoning (graded at low in D-055; moved by D-056)
  - [ ] T-1.14.18.2 `search_plan_queries` on gpt-5.6-luna (graded on gpt-5.4-nano)
  - [ ] T-1.14.18.3 `knowledge_synthesize_report` on claude-sonnet-5 (graded against whatever produced the stored artifact)
  - [ ] T-1.14.18.4 Isolate `qa_refine_context` from the D-055 chain (it is now the only unmeasured step between retrieval and a graded answer)
  - [ ] T-1.14.18.5 `search_rank_and_curate` head-to-head on the persisted candidate pool (S-1.14.6). This makes the slot replayable for the first time
  - [ ] T-1.14.18.6 🔴 Luna on frame selection with captured alternative frames, so agreement can be replaced by graded quality (shares the run with T-1.18.5.5). *Deferred 2026-10-25 with R1 (D-109).*

- S-1.14.19 🟡 Map batches never mix sources; a truncated map response is a failure. Filed 2026-10-19 from R1 run 6. Linked decision: [D-090](decisions.md#d-090--map-batches-never-mix-sources-source-batching-at-a-30k-cap-a-truncated-map-response-is-a-failure-and-map-calls-run-concurrently-2026-10-19). Evidence: [notes/2026-10-19-map-coverage.md](notes/2026-10-19-map-coverage.md). The map model writes about 100 to 140 items per call whatever a batch holds, so sources late in a 120K batch got none (two of ten channels in run 6's annotated arm). Small batches were cut off at a 40% completion budget, and the truncated JSON reached the composer as one unattributed "fact".
  - [x] T-1.14.19.1 A length stop or a non-JSON map response raises `MapOutputError` and goes through bisect-retry; a batch that still fails is dropped and disclosed. Completion budget = the batch cap (ratio 1.0), clamped to the output ceiling
  - [x] T-1.14.19.2 `REPORT_MAP_BATCHING` (`source` / `packed`), `REPORT_MAP_BATCH_TOKENS` (30K), `REPORT_MAP_CONCURRENCY` (4, results merged in batch order)
  - [x] T-1.14.19.3 Measured: `scripts/measure_map_coverage.py`, 2 experiments × 2 arms. With source batching the worst-source density is 0.77 (against 0.00 for the old default) and wrong-script quotes are 0. The pre-registered floor-ratio rule was not met by any config, because the threshold was mis-specified (D-090)
  - [x] T-1.14.19.4 Tests: `test_map_batching.py` (14, mutation-checked: source boundary, length stop, completion order); the three tests that pinned `{"raw": ...}` now pin the failure path
  - [x] T-1.14.19.7 Adversarial review (4 lenses, 3 skeptics per finding): 8 confirmed findings, 4 distinct defects, all fixed. Per-call completion budget, batch cap at 1.5× the output ceiling plus recursive bisect on truncation, backoff on transient errors, a lock on `VideoText`'s matcher. The race test fails without the lock 3 of 3 times
  - [x] T-1.14.19.5 Report-level check (run 7): **failed the substance gate** (1 of 3 families preferred source; 2 ties). Fabrications went the other way: source 0 confirmed, packed 6 (5 of them false exclusivity). The default reverted to `packed` per the rule ([D-094](decisions.md#d-094--map-batching-packed-restored-by-run-7s-pre-registered-rule-starved-sources-re-mapped-alone-the-switch-to-source-is-the-users-call-2026-10-19)); switching to `source` is the user's call
  - [ ] T-1.14.19.6 🔴 Within-source mislabelling: about 1% of `shown`/`both` items have no span from their own source within ±60 s, in every config. Batching cannot fix this one. *Deferred 2026-10-25 with R1 (D-109): the labels matter only while visual spans reach reports.*

- S-1.14.20 🟡 Re-map sources a packed batch starved. Filed 2026-10-19. Linked decision: [D-094](decisions.md#d-094--map-batching-packed-restored-by-run-7s-pre-registered-rule-starved-sources-re-mapped-alone-the-switch-to-source-is-the-users-call-2026-10-19). Linked decision: [D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25) (the default stays; the comparison runs later).
  - [x] T-1.14.20.1 After a packed map pass, a source with ≥10 chunks and <0.2 items per chunk is re-mapped alone (30K calls), and its new items replace the thin ones; `map_sources_remapped` and `map_remap_calls` go into the processing notes
  - [x] T-1.14.20.2 Tests: re-map and replacement, small sources exempt, source mode never re-maps, density rather than presence (mutation-checked)
  - [x] T-1.14.20.3 Map-only measurement: zero-item sources 1→0; floor ratio 0.49/0.72; worst-source density 0.27/0.57; items about 2.4× packed
  - [ ] T-1.14.20.4 🔵 Repeat (rule 2), then a report-level comparison against `source` if the user wants the default decided by measurement. *Ruled 2026-10-25 (D-112): `packed` plus the re-map stays the default, and the comparison runs later. Its pass rule is pre-registered first in the D-107 style (a tie passes, any loss fails) with an explicit fabrication count, and it is batched with S-1.14.18 and S-1.17.4, which share the Gemini 3.1 Pro quota.*

- S-1.14.22 ⚪ Measure the output ceiling of the unmeasured Claude models. Filed 2026-11-05 from PR [#411](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/411); proposed, and the owner's call, because measuring needs a provider call. `max_output_for` gives any model outside `MODEL_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` 4,096, which equals the medium thinking budget, so a `qa_formulate_answer` pin of `claude-opus-4-5:medium` gets `max_tokens=4096`. By `llm_service`'s own notes Claude refuses that. Worked out through the real `_answer_budget`; no call was made. It is on master today and unrelated to clarify, which `max_tokens_for_reply` now protects ([D-138](decisions.md#d-138--code-attempts-count-on-a-shared-row-sockets-end-with-their-session-one-reader-takes-the-client-address-from-the-right-and-a-rotated-key-is-reported-2026-11-05))

**Open questions**
- OQ-7: does the harness generalize to a scheduled regression check (re-run on every model-lineup shift), or stay a manual audit? Depends on S-1.14.6 landing. ✅ **Resolved 2026-10-25 by [D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25):** a manual audit, run by hand whenever a registry default changes.
- OQ-8: judging currently uses a single model family; is a multi-judge panel needed to control for self-preference bias when the challenger shares that family?
- OQ-16 (from S-1.14.7): should a model measured `unacceptable` for a use case be refused at `PUT /settings/llm`, or stay a warning? Today it is a warning everywhere. Refusing protects users from known-bad output; warning keeps the override knob a knob, and the verdicts carry small samples (n=2 on map batches). ✅ **Resolved 2026-10-25 by [D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25):** it stays a warning, because the verdicts rest on n=2.

### E-1.15 🟢 Output length policy (R4) — closed 2026-10-19

**Scope-changed 2026-11-09:** stays 🟢. T-1.15.4.8 and T-1.15.4.9 shipped in PR [#426](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/426) ([D-150](decisions.md#d-150--the-cut-off-flag-is-stored-as-what-the-check-found-a-cut-answer-is-never-a-saved-searchs-baseline-and-a-cut-first-answer-stands-in-on-the-card-2026-11-09)): the cut-off note survives a reload, Q&A history checks for a cut, and a cut saved-search run no longer raises a false alert. T-1.15.3.10 shipped in PR [#423](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/423) ([D-148](decisions.md#d-148--a-401-ends-a-session-in-react-and-keeps-the-page-in-this-tab-a-waiting-share-gets-a-link-a-mutation-that-answers-its-own-failures-says-so-and-tabs-opens-on-focus-2026-11-05)). T-1.15.4.10 is proposed. OQ-39 and OQ-40 are new and the owner's, with OQ-22, OQ-23, OQ-29 and OQ-37. T-1.15.3.6 stays an owner step, and is still the only way to see a real cut.

**Scope-changed 2026-11-05:** stays 🟢. T-1.15.4.5, T-1.15.4.6 and T-1.15.3.9 shipped in PR [#419](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/419) ([D-145](decisions.md#d-145--a-cut-off-flag-is-a-fact-about-one-reply-the-offer-keeps-the-cards-scope-and-the-server-never-applies-the-accounts-default-depth-2026-11-05)), with `stopped_for_length` reading OpenAI's Responses API (T-1.15.4.7). T-1.15.3.10, T-1.15.4.8 and T-1.15.4.9 are proposed. T-1.15.3.6 stays an owner step, and OQ-22, OQ-23, OQ-29 and OQ-37 are the owner's.

**Scope-changed 2026-11-04 (II):** stays 🟢. T-1.15.4.4, T-1.15.3.8 and T-1.15.3.7 shipped in PR [#406](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/406) ([D-132](decisions.md#d-132--qa-brief-keeps-the-whole-thinking-reserve-a-cut-off-report-offers-deep-a-citation-with-no-document-casts-no-vote-and-clarify-is-one-optional-route-2026-11-04)), which departs from [D-125](decisions.md#d-125--a-knowledge-reports-depth-applies-d-064-without-a-bracket-and-belongs-to-the-shared-artifact-and-a-failed-synthesis-never-reaches-it-2026-11-04)'s cap for Q&A brief. T-1.15.3.6, T-1.15.3.9, T-1.15.4.5 and T-1.15.4.6 are proposed, and OQ-22, OQ-23 and OQ-29 are the owner's.

**Scope-changed 2026-11-04:** stays 🟢. [S-1.15.3](#s-1153--length-preference-for-knowledge-synthesis), the one output surface with no user preference, shipped with a new [S-1.15.4](#s-1154--per-ask-answer-depth-on-the-qa-pages) that makes S-1.15.2's per-request override reachable (PR [#395](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/395), [D-125](decisions.md#d-125--a-knowledge-reports-depth-applies-d-064-without-a-bracket-and-belongs-to-the-shared-artifact-and-a-failed-synthesis-never-reaches-it-2026-11-04)). Their follow-ups are proposed, and OQ-22 and OQ-23 are the owner's.

**Why it exists.** User requirement: output length should follow the corpus size bracket, with per-bracket defaults and an optional non-obstructive user override at submission time.
**Linked decision:** [D-064](decisions.md#d-064--output-length-corpus-brackets-with-an-optional-user-override-2026-07-31)

#### S-1.15.1 🟢 Report-side brackets + user override
**Shipped:** 2026-07-31

#### S-1.15.2 🟢 Q&A length policy — agent side shipped 2026-07-31 (D-066); wired into the routers 2026-10-19 (PR [#227](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/227))
Q&A was corpus-blind: `formulate_answer` had a fixed 4,500-token budget and never received video/word counts. Deferred into the R3+R5 batch because all three rewrite the same Q&A prompt files.
**Scope-changed 2026-10-18: reopened.** This story was marked shipped with "R4 now complete", but a code audit found the policy unreachable in production. `run_qa_agent` accepts `corpus_statistics` / `output_length`, but its only caller (`routers/qa.py`) passes neither. `corpus_bracket({})` therefore returns `small`, so every job answer gets the small-corpus 1.25× budget and brief, and no publication span, even on the 200-video corpus. No test references `corpus_statistics`. See the [D-066 amendment](decisions.md#d-066--language-contract-temporal-awareness-and-corpus-aware-qa-length-2026-07-31).
- [x] T-1.15.2.1 Pass corpus statistics into the job and library Q&A agents — `corpus_stats_service` computes them from the approved (job) / visible (library) document set in the routers; both the sync and SSE job endpoints pass them
- [x] T-1.15.2.2 Same scale-plus-guidance policy on both answer sites via `qa_agent._answer_policy`; the library call's fixed `max_tokens=4500` is gone
- [x] T-1.15.2.3 Per-request `output_length` on `QARequest` and `LibraryQARequest`; the request override beats `job.output_length`, NULL everywhere lets the bracket decide
- [x] ~~T-1.15.2.4~~ moved to S-1.15.3 — the knowledge endpoint has no preference input, so "honours a preference" has nothing to test yet
- [x] T-1.15.2.5 `tests/test_routers/test_qa_length_policy.py` asserts at the production caller: the 200K-words-per-document fixture must classify `xlarge`, the rejected document must not count, the override must win, and the library set must follow visibility grants

#### S-1.15.3 🟢 Length preference for knowledge synthesis
Knowledge synthesis already scales its budget with extraction size (S-1.14.8) but has no user preference input. Add an optional `output_length` to `POST /videos/{id}/extract-knowledge`, apply the D-064 multiplier and brief in `synthesize_report`, and test it at the router.
**Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#395](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/395) (batch 4 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-125](decisions.md#d-125--a-knowledge-reports-depth-applies-d-064-without-a-bracket-and-belongs-to-the-shared-artifact-and-a-failed-synthesis-never-reaches-it-2026-11-04). No migration and no new setting.
- [x] T-1.15.3.1 `POST /videos/{id}/extract-knowledge?output_length=auto|brief|standard|deep`, a query parameter beside `?force` because this POST has no body. Extraction does not change. Unset or `auto` is exactly 1.0 with no brief, so the budget and the prompt are byte-identical to S-1.14.8. A single document is deliberately not bracketed (`output_length.override_scale` / `override_guidance`): the bracket would call every document `small`
- [x] T-1.15.3.2 `knowledge_agent.synthesize_report` multiplies S-1.14.8's `max(extraction tokens, 8,000)` by D-064's multiplier (brief 0.45, standard 1.0, deep 1.6) and adds the depth's brief as its own prompt paragraph. A shrunken budget keeps `llm_service.thinking_reserve_tokens` on top of the floor's scaled share, capped at the unset budget (`_synthesis_budget`, a review fix): on a short source brief is 3,600 with reasoning off and 7,696 on the default `claude-sonnet-5:medium`, so the brief does the shortening. A plain 3,600 is under the 4,096 a legacy Claude model budgets at medium, and the API refuses that call
- [x] T-1.15.3.3 The depth used is stored as `_output_length` in `extracted_knowledge_json`; both knowledge endpoints return `output_length` (`null` on older reports), and a displayed report says "Written at deep depth."
- [x] T-1.15.3.4 A run that yields no report changes nothing (a review fix): the router answers 502, leaves the stored artifact as it was and records no usage. Before, any failure blanked the shared report for every tenant and returned 200. A report cut off at its budget is still stored, with a logged warning (`stopped_for_length`, moved from `report_agent` to `llm_service`)
- [x] T-1.15.3.5 `VideoKnowledgeDrawer`: a "Report depth" select (Auto, Brief, Standard, Deep) beside "Generate report" or "Regenerate", starting on the run's `output_length` on the job page and on Auto in the library. **Behaviour change:** the drawer no longer starts a report on open, because a report is metered (10 a month on Free) and a choice offered after a run has started is no choice for the first report. Two tests that pinned "generates on open" now pin the click
- Tests: `test_knowledge_length.py` through the router with only the model faked, `test_output_length.py`, `test_llm_service_reasoning.py`, vitest `knowledgeDrawerRequest`, `jobDetailKnowledge`, `videoRowKnowledge` and `depth`; 8 backend and 21 frontend mutations, all caught. The brief depth has not run on a live model.
- [ ] T-1.15.3.6 ⚪ *(proposed 2026-11-04, from #395; an owner step)* One live look: generate a brief and a deep knowledge report on a short source and confirm the brief one is not truncated *2026-11-04 (#406): ask one brief question on the default model too, to see the real length under T-1.15.4.4's budget.* *2026-11-05 (#419): no answer has been seen to stop at its budget on a live model, so this look can also confirm the cut-off note appears.*
- [x] T-1.15.3.7 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-04, from #395)* The channel page's `BatchExtract` sends no depth and keeps today's behaviour. A per-batch depth is a separate UX decision
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#406](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/406) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-132](decisions.md#d-132--qa-brief-keeps-the-whole-thinking-reserve-a-cut-off-report-offers-deep-a-citation-with-no-document-casts-no-vote-and-clarify-is-one-optional-route-2026-11-04). `BatchExtract` has one depth select at the confirm step, on Auto, and the same depth goes to every document in the batch. Auto sends no parameter, so that request is byte-identical to before.

  **Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#406](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/406) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). The submit form's Topic tab, the knowledge drawer and the Q&A panels start from it through a subscriber to the existing `/auth/me` query, so no page gains a request. The server never applies the value. Auto stays NULL and sends nothing, and a depth picked on a page wins.
- [x] T-1.15.3.10 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-05, from #419)* `BatchExtract` does not start from the account's default depth. The spec names the submit form, drawer and Q&A panels, and a batch spends one metered report per document
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#423](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/423) (batch 7 of 2026-11-05).
