  **Shipped:** 2026-11-10 — PR [#452](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/452) (batch 12 of 2026-11-10). Linked decision: [D-167](decisions.md#d-167--a-querys-toast-stands-aside-per-mounted-reader-a-reader-may-say-every-failure-or-only-a-first-load-and-the-guard-holds-each-condition-to-a-page-test-2026-11-10). The `QueryCache`'s `onError` asks `queryFailureShownOnPage(query)`, which reads the readers mounted when the failure lands (`query.observers`), not `query.meta`, which is only the last reader to render. The toast stands aside when an enabled reader carries `meta: ERRORS_SHOWN_HERE`, or the new `LOAD_ERRORS_SHOWN_HERE` while the query has no data. So a key read by a page with a line and by a silent component, such as the shelves page and the sidebar, still toasts where the silent one is mounted alone, and a page left mid-load leaves the failure to the toast. New helpers in `services/queryClient.ts`: `InlineErrors` (`boolean | 'load'`), `QueryLineOptions` and `queryErrorsMeta()`. About 40 query readers in about 45 files follow the rule. Hooks with one reader that always shows a line set the meta in the hook. The graph, the report peek, Echo's data card and readiness, the run page and the knowledge drawer use `'load'`, since they keep their data on screen when a refresh fails. Sixteen hooks whose readers differ take `{ inlineErrors }`, some worked out from what is on screen: the library's visible tab, the two-step card's setup step, the cost panel, a book's framing. The palette's search stands aside only when no action matches, since only then does its line show. Each line gives the server's words through `inlineErrorMessage`, else its sentence, and nothing on a 401. **Copy changes:** the run page says "Couldn't load this run." for a failure that is not a 404, and a 404 keeps "We can't find that job."; the knowledge and report readers no longer show axios's text; lines with no alert role gained `role="alert"`. On pages that show a line over their data, the runs list and the system page, a failed poll is no longer toasted every interval, and a run page that 404s no longer toasts every 5 seconds. The guard (`oneToastPerFailure.test.ts`) gains a fourth half, per reader: a reader that builds a line from a query's failure must stand aside, and one that stands aside must show a line; hiding the component, navigating away or a call that keeps no result is not a line. `QUERY_NOT_YET` is empty. **Review fixes:** a reader that stands aside only under a condition had no test of when, so `loadFailuresConditions.test.tsx` checks each condition both ways (the line once and no toast, or the toast and no line), and the guard lists every such file in `CONDITIONAL_READERS` beside its test, failing on an unlisted file or a missing test; `jobDetailLoadFailure.test.tsx` checks the run page with the real `useJob`; and the palette's live status no longer repeats a failed search that the toast or the alert already says. Tested under `createQueryClient()` with retries as the app has them, with a failure with words, a 500, no answer and a 401; the mutation checks are in the test files' headers. `npx vitest run` passed 133 files and 1,888 tests, and the device suite passed with no baseline changed. CLAUDE.md's "One toast per failure" bullet says queries follow the rule. Frontend only. The console error `job.preferred_channels.join is not a function` seen during the shell spec comes from its own test of the error screen, which feeds a broken run on purpose. Left out: polls that toast on every failure with no line (T-3.4.1.27).
- [x] T-3.4.1.27 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-10, from #452)* A polling query whose reader shows no line toasts on every failed poll. `useSystemStatus` in `AppLayout` toasts a failing `/health` every 60 seconds, although its comment says health errors stay quiet, and the run page toasts a poll that fails behind a run already on screen every 5 seconds, as before #452. Silencing the health poll would say the failure nowhere, so it needs its own decision, such as one toast per run of failures. Seen and left in #452: a 403 on a refresh while Echo's readiness is on screen shows the upgrade card and the toast both, which takes a plan change while the page is open
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-10 — PR [#458](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/458) (batch 13 of 2026-11-10). Linked decision: [D-170](decisions.md#d-170--a-poll-says-a-run-of-failures-once-the-streak-is-the-caches-own-record-a-page-may-take-a-failure-no-reader-says-and-the-guard-lists-every-poll-2026-11-10). The `QueryCache` holds a poll to one toast per run of failures (`PollFailureStreaks` in `services/queryClient.ts`). A query is a poll when a mounted, enabled reader has a `refetchInterval` that resolves to a positive number now (`queryIsPolling`). A streak starts with the first failure the toast says and ends when a fetch succeeds (the cache's `onSuccess`, which `setQueryData` never calls) or when the poll's last reader unmounts, so a return to a page whose poll still fails says it once more. Within a streak each kind of failure is said once: the HTTP status, or no answer (`pollFailureKind`), never the words. A failure a line said is not counted as said. A 401 still says nothing, and a query that does not poll still toasts every failure. Checked first against TanStack 5.95.2, in a test, that the query's own state cannot mark a streak: the failure is written before the cache's `onError` runs, a fetch with no data clears the error when it starts, and `dataUpdateCount` moves on every `setQueryData`, which the run page's socket calls on each progress line. Polls covered: the health check, `useJob`, `useJobs`, the bell, the frames panel, the importer and the voice test; `useSavedSearches` and the system page's three polls always stand aside for their own line, so the streak never applies there. `useSystemStatus`'s stale comment is replaced. **Echo 403, reworked on review:** with the Echo page open, the sections the upgrade card replaces, and the page's own readers when the account read answers first and disables them, toasted their 403 beside the card (seven toasts on the first commit). The query client now lets a page take a failure no mounted reader says while it is open (`sayFailuresOnPage`). The Echo page takes every 403 on an `['echo', ...]` read for its card (`useEchoPlanRefusal` in `hooks/useEcho.ts`), since every Echo route's one 403 is the Studio gate, and shows the card from the cache's `updated` event, which TanStack sends before `onError`, so no order of answers leaves a toast beside it. A status read that succeeds again takes the card away; a 500 still toasts, and a 403 that lands once the page has gone toasts as before. The first commit's `ShownOnPage` form of `inlineErrors` is gone. The guard (`oneToastPerFailure.test.ts`) gains a fifth part: every `refetchInterval`, written out, short, spread or assigned, is listed in `POLLS` beside its tests, and nothing polls by a timer of its own (a `setInterval` that asks a query again, inline, named or a member reference, or a `setTimeout` loop; a one-shot debounce is allowed). Tested under `createQueryClient()` with the app's retries in `pollFailures.test.tsx`, `healthPoll.test.tsx` (the real `AppLayout`), `pollHooks.test.tsx` (one row per polling hook), `echoPlanRefusal.test.tsx` (every real Echo section, each order of answers), and extended `jobDetailLoadFailure`, `loadFailuresConditions` and `systemStatusPage` tests; the mutation checks are in each file's header. `npx vitest run` passed 138 files and 1,962 tests, and the device suite passed with no baseline changed. CLAUDE.md's "One toast per failure" bullet gains a passage on polls. Frontend only, no owner step. Left out: the guard cannot read a timer callback passed through a prop or defined in another module, and none exists today.

**Open questions (from PR #400, the owner's):**
- OQ-24: does a failed test write the gate? The spec's test list says written on a pass and not on a fail, but it also says to write as `--record` does, and `--record` stores fails too. The app follows `--record`: a fail stores `status="failed"` and leaves the feature off, so a failed retake closes a gate that had passed. The page warns about that before a retake starts.
- OQ-25: should the gate tighten beyond `user-score`? The pre-registration also asks that the quality guard holds for the same drafts, and for 40 live notes *in the language tested*. The harness checks neither, so the app does not either.
- OQ-26: `ECHO_VOICE_TEST_NOTES_MIN` against the 40-note floor. The app uses the pre-registered 40 as a constant, as the harness does, so lowering the setting changes the readiness text but not the test.
- OQ-27: tells in the pairs. Notes are shown raw, Markdown included, at full length beside 40 to 90 word drafts. That matches the harness and pushes toward a fail, so it was left.
- OQ-28: stale tests. A test with no progress for 30 minutes reads as failed. Once the worker picks a test up the clock runs from its last pair, but a test still queued counts from `created_at`, so with `--pool=solo` a long report ahead of it can fail it before it starts. Nothing is spent, but the start counts toward the daily limit.
- OQ-35 (from T-3.4.1.11): [D-137](decisions.md#d-137--a-share-target-posts-to-a-worker-scoped-to-one-path-and-waits-for-one-tap-mail-is-unverified-text-that-never-trains-the-voice-shared-links-go-through-the-public-address-guard-2026-11-04) point 4 says citing only mail "leaves it `ungrounded`". #416 read that as "mail grounds nothing", so a mail-only claiming reply is now refused and the refusal names the email. If mail-only drafts should be delivered with the flag instead, that is one condition in `finalize` and one in `retrieve_personal_context`. This touches OQ-30. The owner's call.

What shipped:
- **Gates, in this order, each in words:** the install switch, readiness v2 ([D-101](decisions.md#d-101--echo-readiness-v2-five-domains-measured-from-their-stores-blocked-reads-as-not-connected-the-voice-test-reported-not-gated-2026-10-20)), the voice test (not run or failed), the daily cap. A Studio tier is needed before any of them.
- **Guards in code:**
  - Third-party runs of 8 words are paraphrased, then dropped.
  - Exemplars come from own words only.
  - Private names stop the draft until the user acknowledges them.
  - Citations are the markers the draft used.
  - Grounding is flagged when a question, opinion or recommendation cites nothing.
- **Language:** the draft is in the reply's language, not D-066 English.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_services/test_echo_draft.py` (18) and `tests/test_routers/test_echo_drafts.py` (7) cover the agent, the guards, every gate, the names round trip, the 429, tenant isolation and validation. The privacy round trip covers the two new tables. `speakAsMe.test.tsx` (7) covers the card.
- **Live smoke:** run on a synthetic persona at about $0.02 to $0.05 a draft.

#### S-3.4.2 🟢 Feedback loop and limits
**Shipped:** 2026-10-20 — PR [#359](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/359). **Linked decision:** [D-104](decisions.md#d-104--a-draft-edit-teaches-only-the-words-the-user-typed-2026-10-20). Already in S-3.4.1: the quota key (`echo_drafts`, daily, Studio 20), the disclosure line on every draft, the `echo_drafted` audit event and the own-words-only exemplars.
- [x] T-3.4.2.1 `POST /echo/drafts/{id}/feedback {final_text}` stores the diff as a `voice_signals` candidate with `derived_from=draft_edit`
- [x] T-3.4.2.2 `ECHO_DRAFTS_PER_DAY` quota key through `quota_metering_service`; a visible, user-removable disclosure line ("Drafted with Echo, reviewed by <user>") on by default, the "reviewed by" clause being what the EU AI Act Art. 50(4) editorial-responsibility exception keys on if a draft is ever published; `audit_log` event per draft; the provenance guard (only the tenant's own authored text feeds a voice) enforced in code, which makes impersonating anyone else structurally impossible
- [x] T-3.4.2.3 Tests: quota; feedback creates a signal

What shipped:
- **Feedback.** `POST /echo/drafts/{id}/feedback {final_text, disclosure_kept}` stores the user's version and whether the disclosure stayed. It strips a trailing disclosure line and may be sent again. It is not counted against the cap and is audited as `echo_draft_feedback` with counts.
- **Learning from the edit.** Only the words the user TYPED (`echo_draft_edits`) become the extraction input `draft_edit:<id>`; the draft words they kept never do. Nor does anything the user repeats from the incoming message: S-3.4.1's 8-word guard sets it aside as `quoted`, even when typed and even across kept draft words. An edit of fewer than 4 typed words is not an input. Edits do not feed cadence, and deleting the draft withdraws them at once.
- **The card and the bubble.** Both use one `DraftEditor`. The draft is an editable "Your version", with an **Add the disclosure line** checkbox (on by default; Copy follows it) and **Save my version**, which says how many of the user's words Echo will learn from and that the sender's words are left out.
- **Tests:**
  - `test_echo_draft_feedback.py` (13) covers the diff, the incoming-message guard (including a quote across kept words and the 8-word threshold), the disclosure, storage, the audit, ownership, the input, a run that learns only from typed words and never sees the sender's, withdrawal, and the provenance guard across tenants, context-only shares and drafts.
  - Also a router test and a column round trip.
  - `speakAsMe.test.tsx` has 4 more, including the shared-view wiring, and `echoPanel.test.tsx` saves from the bubble.
- **Live check:** a real draft, edited, unticked and saved. The extraction learned two signals whose only example is the clause the user typed.

### E-3.5 🟢 Cold-start readiness threshold

**Scope.** Quantify "enough data has been ingested to safely activate Echo features" and gate Ring 3 surface behind this threshold.
**Shipped 2026-05-05** in PR #172. `echo_service.is_ready(db, user_id, total_threshold=100, sources_threshold=3)` returns an `EchoReadiness` dataclass: `(ready, total_rows, distinct_sources, has_personality_trait, threshold_total, threshold_sources)`. Default thresholds are conservative (100 rows / 3 sources / ≥1 personality_trait); operators tune via the function args. Endpoint `GET /api/v1/echo/status` exposes the readiness diagnostics so the frontend can show "you're 60% there" progress UI. *Built 2026-10-19 on `/echo` (PR #224): the percentage is the mean of the three gates, capped at 99 until `ready`, each gate drawn against its threshold ([D-069](decisions.md#d-069--shipped-backend-surfaces-ingest-as-a-library-tab-author-studio-as-one-page-readiness-as-a-gate-mean-2026-10-19)).*

**Scope-changed 2026-10-18:** 🟢 → 🟡. [D-049](decisions.md#d-049--echo-cold-start-readiness-threshold-shipped-triple-is-v1-personal-brainmd-sub-table-is-forward-looking-spec-resolves-oq-6-2026-05-06) named the four-domain table in `personal-brain.md` as the forward-looking spec; once shares, events and signals exist (E-3.7, E-3.2, E-3.3) the v1 triple is no longer the right gate.

**Shipped 2026-10-20** in PR [#354](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/354): readiness v2, five domains measured from their stores (S-3.5.1). The v1 triple is still reported but no longer gates. **Linked decision:** [D-101](decisions.md#d-101--echo-readiness-v2-five-domains-measured-from-their-stores-blocked-reads-as-not-connected-the-voice-test-reported-not-gated-2026-10-20) (supersedes D-049).

### E-3.6 🟢 Tier gating (Studio-only initially)

**Shipped 2026-05-05** in PR #172. Two new feature flags: `echo_personal_brain` (gates the entire `/echo/*` surface) + `echo_speak_as_me` (gates the future "speak as me" agent — E-3.4). Both are Studio-tier-only at the `TIER_CAPABILITIES` level. Frontend shows the Echo surface only when `has_feature("echo_personal_brain")` returns true.

### E-3.7 🟡 Constant-stream intake (Domain 5)

**Progress 2026-11-04:** S-3.7.6 and S-3.7.4's self-host half (T-3.7.4.1) shipped in PR [#407](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/407) ([D-137](decisions.md#d-137--a-share-target-posts-to-a-worker-scoped-to-one-path-and-waits-for-one-tap-mail-is-unverified-text-that-never-trains-the-voice-shared-links-go-through-the-public-address-guard-2026-11-04)). This brings the epic's first approval step: a share from the share target waits for one tap, because any website can post that form. The extension and the Shortcut keep adding at once, since a token in a header proves who is asking. E-3.7 stays 🟡 for T-3.7.4.2 (SaaS) and the real-device and real-mailbox checks.

**Progress 2026-10-19.** S-3.7.1 shipped with the privacy pair S-3.10.1 / S-3.10.2 ([#286](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/286)). Processing (S-3.7.2) is next.
**Linked decision:** [D-089](decisions.md#d-089--echos-privacy-contract-context-only-by-default-the-brain-is-a-registry-the-archive-is-the-users-whole-copy-delete-in-a-fixed-order-2026-10-19) (context-only by default, the brain as a registry, the whole archive, delete order).

**Filed 2026-10-18.** The roadmap's first-to-ship Echo component: the user forwards liked videos, reels, memes, WhatsApp threads, Keep notes, quotes, bookmarks and voice memos all day with no approval step, because the act of sharing is the curation. Everything lands in one `shares_inbox`, is processed into text, embedded, and cross-feeds facts (E-3.1), signals (E-3.3), events (E-3.2) and, when the share is an ingestible source, the library through the normal ingest path.
**Plan:** [`docs/plans/l3-echo-personal-brain.md`](plans/l3-echo-personal-brain.md) § Domain 5
**Acceptance.** A user pastes a YouTube URL, drops a screenshot of a meme, and uploads a WhatsApp export; within a minute each appears in the inbox with extracted text, the video is offered to the library, the meme's text is searchable, the WhatsApp thread shows a "redact the other party" option; readiness moves; deleting a share removes its derived signals.

#### S-3.7.1 🟢 `shares_inbox`, quick-share and the inbox page
**PR:** [#286](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/286) — merged 2026-10-19, together with S-3.10.1 and S-3.10.2 so the first new Echo table arrived with its export and erasure.
**Calls made in the PR.** (1) **Third-party content is context-only by default**, the plan's recommendation for its open question 2: `author_is_tenant` is false unless the user ticks "These are my own words"; a context-only share may be retrieved as context once S-3.7.2 embeds it, but is never quoted and never enters a dataset or derived export. Marking one's own words is authorship, not an override; the per-thread override the open question asks about was not built. (2) Every share starts `pending`, text included: its words are stored as `extracted_text` at once, but embedding and signals are S-3.7.2's. (3) The stored file name is the share id plus a sanitised extension, never the client's name; downloads are attachments with `nosniff`; a row whose path points outside its tenant directory is never followed. (4) `linked_document_id` instead of the sketch's `linked_source_id` (documents are the library's unit), and `updated_at` added.

#### S-3.7.2 🟢 Processing pipeline
**PR:** part 1 [#324](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/324) — merged 2026-10-19: the `echo.process_share` task (or inline, or off), text and links (YouTube through the Data API, everything else through the article extractor), text files, embedding into `shares_global`, the library offer, Retry, fail-soft. Part 2 [#331](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/331) — merged 2026-10-19: images, voice memos, video clips and PDFs ([measurements](notes/2026-10-19-s372-share-ocr-measurement.md)). **Calls made in part 2.** (1) *Local-only, made concrete:* a tenant is local-only when every other use case resolves to `local` through the install's config or their own overrides; for them, and when the new `ECHO_REMOTE_MEDIA` is off, nothing goes to a remote vision model or to Whisper, and the share is `skipped` with a note saying why. A vision model the install runs locally is always allowed. Processing runs inside the tenant's `byok_context`, so their keys and overrides are the ones used. (2) *No Devanagari model yet:* RapidOCR's default models cannot read Devanagari (measured: Hindi read nothing or noise), so Hindi images go to the vision fallback, which read them at 0.93-1.00. A local-only tenant's Hindi images are therefore unread until a Devanagari recognition model is added. (3) *New use case `echo_share_ocr`* on `gpt-5.4-mini` at reasoning off; it tied `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` (0.979 against 0.983 on four images) and every install has an OpenAI key. (4) *No library offer for PDFs:* the library's PDF ingest lives inside its upload endpoint, and two other lanes are editing that router; the PDF is kept whole in the archive. (5) Extracted text is capped at `ECHO_SHARE_TEXT_MAX_CHARS` with a note; the file stays whole. **Shipped:** 2026-10-19. **Calls made in part 1.** A YouTube link gets no library offer: there is no single-video library ingest, and a research run is the way in. Processing results live in a new `shares_inbox.processing_json` (migration `65ce471f8571`), so `raw_payload_json` stays exactly what arrived. One vector per share, like notes: the annotation, the title and the opening of the text (`ECHO_SHARE_EMBED_MAX_CHARS`).

- [x] T-3.7.2.2 Embedding into a `shares_global` collection with `tenant_id`; shares resolving to a source are offered to the library (one-click ingest through the paste path, visibility granted to the sharer only)

The voice gate is already there: `voice_eligible(share)` is true only for the user's own words, and the vector metadata carries it *Closed 2026-11-09 (batch 8 census): both targets shipped, T-3.2.1.9 in PR [#332](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/332) and T-3.3.2.6 in PR [#335](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/335). `echo_processing_service.process_share` records the share event and queues signal extraction.*

- [x] T-3.7.2.5 Tests per content type with external calls patched (`tests/test_routers/test_echo_processing.py`, `tests/test_routers/test_echo_media.py`)

The extraction itself still gets the whole URL, since trafilatura reads a date from the path. Podcast URLs are T-1.7.1.6.

#### S-3.7.3 🟢 Drag-drop and browser extension
**Shipped:** 2026-10-19 — PR [#348](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/348); closed without T-3.7.3.3, which is deferred ([#352](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/352)) and, since 2026-10-25, won't-do ([D-110](decisions.md#d-110--echo-only-sees-what-the-user-hands-it-no-background-capture-and-the-echo-scope-rulings-2026-10-25)).
**PR:** part 1 [#348](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/348) — merged 2026-10-19: the drop zone, share tokens, and the `extension/` Manifest V3 extension (context menus, toolbar popup, settings), quotes and images with their source page, and images shared by address fetched and read on the server. ~~**Part 2 (next):** T-3.7.3.3, history capture through lane B's connector runtime.~~ Not built; see T-3.7.3.3. **Calls made in part 1.** (1) *Share tokens, not sessions:* the app has no sessions page, and a session expires within hours, so the extension uses a **share token**. It lasts until revoked, it is accepted only by the share intake routes and the connection check, it cannot read anything or mint another, and only its SHA-256 hash is stored, in `echo_share_tokens` (a D-089 secret table: erased with the brain, never exported). Tokens are made and revoked on the Echo page. (2) *Images go by address:* fetching image bytes in the extension needs host permission for every site, so the extension sends the image URL and the server fetches it. The server refuses hosts that resolve to private, loopback or link-local addresses, re-checks every redirect, accepts only image content types and stops at `ECHO_SHARE_MAX_BYTES`, then reads the image like an upload. (3) *Quotes keep their page:* `source_url` and `source_title` on a share; a quote stays context only (D-089). (4) *Exports dropped anywhere* open the importer with the file already previewed.
- [x] T-3.7.3.1 Drop zone on every page (files, URLs, text) posting to `/echo/shares` with `surface=drag_drop`
- [x] T-3.7.3.2 *(token from the Echo page, not a sessions page; see the calls above)* Manifest V3 extension (`extension/`): context-menu "Share to Pratidhvani" on page, link, selection and image; token pasted from the app's sessions page; posts with `surface=browser_ext`
- ~~T-3.7.3.3 Optional history capture in the extension (per-domain allow-list, never incognito) lands as `activity_events` through the connector runtime; opt-in per category~~ ✖ Won't do 2026-10-25: Echo only sees what the user hands it (D-110). The design below stays in the plan for the record.
  **Blocked-on:** user decision (Echo privacy promise; [plan open question 7](plans/l3-echo-personal-brain.md#open-questions-the-users-call)). The plan rejects passive browsing capture, and explicit capture covers the need. **Preferred approach if the user opts in:** per-domain optional host permissions (Chrome asks for each site as the user adds it, so no `history` permission and no install warning); URL, title and time only, never page content; `incognito: not_allowed` in the manifest; domains grouped into categories the user can pause; visits posted with the share token to a write-only route that refuses without an active `browser_history` grant, which needs a third, "push" connector kind in S-3.2.1's runtime (consent screen, no OAuth, disconnect deletes the events). Recorded in the [plan](plans/l3-echo-personal-brain.md#domain-5--shares-inbox-e-37).
  **Scope-changed 2026-10-19:** deferred, and S-3.7.3 closed without it.
  **Scope-changed 2026-10-25:** won't-do. The owner chose "Echo sees only what I hand it" ([D-110](decisions.md#d-110--echo-only-sees-what-the-user-hands-it-no-background-capture-and-the-echo-scope-rulings-2026-10-25)), which settles the blocker above against capture.
- [x] T-3.7.3.4 🟢 *(proposed 2026-10-25, from PR [#378](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/378)'s suite runs)* `test_echo_share_tokens.py::test_tokens_are_capped_and_last_use_is_recorded` is flaky, on master too (1 failure in 10 runs): the token list orders by `created_at` and then a random id, so two tokens made within one clock tick come back in either order. Give the list a deterministic tie-break, or give the test's tokens distinct `created_at` values. A test fix; S-3.7.3 stays 🟢. *Seen again 2026-10-29 in the full-suite runs that landed #384, #385 and #386; #386's lander measured 3 failures in 5 isolated runs on Windows.* *Seen again 2026-11-04 in #388's local full run (passes alone; CI passed).*
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#410](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/410) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-136](decisions.md#d-136--a-books-contents-take-their-ids-from-the-rendered-manuscript-the-main-chunk-meets-its-budget-with-lazy-markdown-and-lazy-routes-and-the-device-suites-server-is-dual-stack-2026-11-04). Two tokens made inside one clock tick tie on `created_at`, and the tie-break is a random uuid. The test now finds the token by id, which also checks the other token was not touched, and a new test pins newest-first with `created_at` set by hand. No product change. With every token forced into one tick the old test failed 54 of 100 and the fixed one 0 of 100, and on the real clock the fixed test passed 300 of 300.

#### S-3.7.4 🟡 Email-in inbox
**Progress 2026-11-04:** the self-host half shipped (T-3.7.4.1). T-3.7.4.2 waits for SaaS, so the story stays open.
*2026-10-25 ([D-110](decisions.md#d-110--echo-only-sees-what-the-user-hands-it-no-background-capture-and-the-echo-scope-rulings-2026-10-25)): the self-host answer to email, since Gmail (T-3.2.3) stays off SaaS before revenue.*
- [x] T-3.7.4.1 Self-host: IMAP poller for a dedicated mailbox (settings `ECHO_IMAP_*`), each message a share with `surface=email_in`
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#407](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/407) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-137](decisions.md#d-137--a-share-target-posts-to-a-worker-scoped-to-one-path-and-waits-for-one-tap-mail-is-unverified-text-that-never-trains-the-voice-shared-links-go-through-the-public-address-guard-2026-11-04). `ECHO_IMAP_*` settings, off by default, and `app/services/echo_imap_service.py` over verified TLS, polled from the single scheduler tick through one `scheduled_runs` row of kind `echo_imap`, with no second beat entry. Each message from an allowed sender becomes one text share with `surface=email_in`. An empty allow list accepts nothing and the mailbox is not opened. A sender not on the list is dropped on headers alone, and a From must be one well-formed address in a header block that parsed whole. Mail is always a text share and attachments are listed, not read, so a forged message cannot make the server fetch or spend. Mail can never be marked as the user's own words, on create or `PATCH`, and in a draft it is labelled an unverified email, with `unverified_source` set in code. `echo_email_messages` (migration `c62da1d081c6`, in `ECHO_TABLES`) commits with the share, so a re-poll adds nothing and a deleted share stays deleted. Logs carry counts and exception types only. SPF, DKIM and DMARC are not checked and STARTTLS is not offered; `docs/echo-share-surfaces.md` states the limits. Tests: `test_echo_email_inbox.py`, at the scheduler tick, the Celery task and the inbox route, with one test of the real `imaplib` against a scripted server over a loopback socket.

Send from an allowed address and from one that is not, then check that one share appears and that the other mail stays in the mailbox, read and with no share. Mark the first mail unread and check that nothing is added

#### S-3.7.5 🟢 Export-file importers
**Shipped:** 2026-10-19 — PRs [#341](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/341) and [#349](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/349).
**PR:** part 1 [#341](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/341) — merged 2026-10-19: WhatsApp chats and Google Keep notes as shares, through importers that meet S-3.2.1's `ArchiveImporter` contract and register in its registry (`app/connectors/whatsapp/`, `app/connectors/google_keep/`; shared helpers and the optional `sniff` / `preview` extras in `app/connectors/archive.py`), `/echo/import` with preview, undo, and the import page ([sizing](notes/2026-10-19-s375-import-sizing.md)). The only route to several high-value signals. **Part 2 (next):** the event importers (YouTube Takeout watch and search history, Spotify, Goodreads, Letterboxd, Strava) against lane B's `activity_events` writer (S-3.2.1), and Apple Health into `personal_context`. **Calls made in part 1.** (1) A WhatsApp chat is stored as **turns**: consecutive messages from one sender at most `ECHO_IMPORT_TURN_GAP_MIN` (10) apart, up to `ECHO_IMPORT_TURN_MAX_CHARS` (2,000). Authorship is uniform within a share, and retrieval gets units longer than "ok". (2) The user says which participant they are at preview; with no choice, everything is context only. (3) Imports get a record of their own (`echo_imports`, registered in D-089's registry) and every imported share carries `import_id` and `occurred_at` (migration `509a4efd9319`), so one import can be undone and an import reads oldest first. (4) The upload is never stored: parsing streams the spooled request, so "keep only my side" means the other side never reaches the database. (5) The inbox defaults to shares handed over one at a time; imports are a filter and a per-import view. (6) Keep notes default to the user's own words, with a switch. (7) S-3.2.1 shipped the registry kind (T-3.2.1.7) while this was in flight, so these importers adopted it rather than keep a second registry: detection tries each importer's optional `sniff` (content) before `accepts` (filename), and a filename rule claims only the service's own export names, so a plain `notes.json` matches nothing. Each item carries a stable `external_id` for de-duplicated re-imports later.
**Part 2:** [#349](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/349) — merged 2026-10-19: Google Takeout (Keep notes plus YouTube watch and search history), Spotify, Goodreads, Letterboxd and Strava as `activity_events`, and Apple Health as three `daily_routine` facts, each a package under `app/connectors/` registered in S-3.2.1's registry; `backend/scripts/echo_import.py` for exports past the upload cap ([sizing, part 2](notes/2026-10-19-s375-import-sizing.md#part-2--event-importers-and-apple-health)). **Calls made in part 2.** (1) *Events go through S-3.2.1's writer* with `connector` set to the importer's name, and only events not already held are written, each payload marked `_import: <id>`. Undo then removes exactly what that import added, and next year's export of the same history adds only the new tail. (2) *One Takeout importer:* an archive holds Keep and YouTube together, and one import record should undo one archive, so `google_takeout` reads both (and the connector is `google_takeout`, not the `youtube_takeout` named below); `google_keep` keeps the single-note JSON files. (3) *The user's words in an event are `text_content`:* Goodreads reviews and private notes, Letterboxd reviews, Strava descriptions and YouTube searches, the field voice signals are to read (E-3.3 does not read events yet). Titles and metadata are payload. (4) *Left out at the parser:* Spotify private-session plays, the IP address and country on every play, YouTube ads, Strava routes and GPS files (never opened), and Apple Health's records themselves. (5) *Apple Health is three facts,* not events: sleep (median hours, typical bedtime and wake time), steps and workouts a week, over `window_days` (default 365), confidence 0.8, `source: import:<id>`. Undo removes the ones still carrying that source. A watch and a phone both record steps and sleep, so the larger source wins each day instead of the sum. A watch's sleep-stage segments join into sessions when under 90 minutes apart, and a session needs three hours asleep to count as a night. (6) *Strava* data is the user's own, from the bulk export, which the API agreement does not cover; the plan's note to confirm with counsel before SaaS launch stands. (7) S-3.2.1's writer inserts new rows in bulk and takes a `batch_size` (its default unchanged); the importers commit every 1,000 rows. Measured: 196k Spotify plays import in 33 s (was 53 s); a five-year Apple Health export (2.4M records) parses in 23 s at a flat 50 MB.
- [x] T-3.7.5.1 WhatsApp `.txt` export parser (date / sender / message lines, media placeholders), with "redact other party" producing a sender-only copy before storage
- [x] T-3.7.5.2 *(connector `google_takeout`: one importer for the whole archive; see call 2 above)* Google Takeout: YouTube watch and search history JSON → `activity_events` (`connector=youtube_takeout`); Keep notes JSON → shares. **Keep half done in part 1**; the YouTube half waits for `activity_events`, and the Keep preview already names it when a Takeout archive holds it
- [x] T-3.7.5.3 Spotify "Extended streaming history" JSON → events (the complete source; the API is a 50-item tail); Goodreads and Letterboxd CSV → `read` / `watched` events; Strava bulk export → workout events (user-owned data outside Strava's API policy; confirm with counsel per the plan); Apple Health `export.xml` → `daily_routine` facts via a streaming parser (exports run to gigabytes)
- [x] T-3.7.5.6 `author_is_tenant` flag on every imported message: third-party text (the other side of a WhatsApp thread) is retrievable as context, never quoted verbatim in outputs and excluded from dataset exports
- [x] T-3.7.5.4 Upload endpoint with format detection; streaming parse so a 100 MB export does not load into memory (measured: an 89 MB chat parses in 19 s with a flat heap)
- [x] T-3.7.5.5 *(part 2: `tests/test_routers/test_echo_event_imports.py`, 21 tests)* Tests on fixture exports for every format. **WhatsApp (iOS, Android, US locale, ambiguous dates, zip) and Keep done in part 1** (`tests/test_routers/test_echo_imports.py`, fixtures in `tests/fixtures/echo_imports/`)
- [x] T-3.7.5.7 Undo an import: every share it wrote, their vectors and anything derived, through the same bulk cascade as a single delete (`echo_intake_service.delete_shares`)

#### S-3.7.6 🟢 OS share target
- [x] T-3.7.6.1 Web-app manifest `share_target` (depends on S-8.12.2) so Android shares land in the inbox; iOS via a Shortcut that posts to `/echo/shares`

Linked decision: [D-137](decisions.md#d-137--a-share-target-posts-to-a-worker-scoped-to-one-path-and-waits-for-one-tap-mail-is-unverified-text-that-never-trains-the-voice-shared-links-go-through-the-public-address-guard-2026-11-04). The manifest gains a `POST` multipart `share_target`, and `frontend/public/share-target-sw.js` is a service worker scoped to `/echo/share-target` alone: it keeps the form in the Cache API and redirects to `/echo/incoming`, with nothing in the address, and controls no page. `EchoShareTargetPage` shows what arrived and posts with `surface=os_share` on one tap, covering only what it showed, because any website can post that form (a blocker the review found). A share made signed out waits on the device through any sign-in, expires after 24 hours and is dropped at sign-out. iPhone: the Shortcut's steps are in `docs/echo-share-surfaces.md` and on the Echo page, and the requests they build are tested at `/echo/shares`. A shared link's fetch now goes through the public-address guard on every share surface, redirects included. Device suite: `frontend/tests/manifest.spec.ts`, with a real multipart navigation, a cross-origin form and a reload. **Behaviour change:** this is the one approval step in Echo's intake.

Android: over HTTPS, install from Chrome (reinstall if it is already installed, since Chrome picks up a new `share_target` only when it updates the app), share a page, some text and an image, tap Add to Echo, then share while signed out, and check that the address bar and the web server's access log hold no shared text. iPhone: build the Shortcut from the Echo page's steps. They have never run on an iPhone, so a menu name may differ. This can share a sitting with S-8.12.3's real-phone pass
- [x] T-3.7.6.3 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-05, from #414)* With a deep link while a share is waiting, the reader goes to the deep link and the share waits, up to 24 hours, for the next visit to `/`
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#423](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/423) (batch 7 of 2026-11-05). Linked decision: [D-148](decisions.md#d-148--a-401-ends-a-session-in-react-and-keeps-the-page-in-this-tab-a-waiting-share-gets-a-link-a-mutation-that-answers-its-own-failures-says-so-and-tabs-opens-on-focus-2026-11-05). `WaitingShareNotice`, a line in `AppLayout` with a **Review it** link, on every page but `/` and the review page. It adds no redirect ([D-137](decisions.md#d-137--a-share-target-posts-to-a-worker-scoped-to-one-path-and-waits-for-one-tap-mail-is-unverified-text-that-never-trains-the-voice-shared-links-go-through-the-public-address-guard-2026-11-04) point 9), and nothing is posted until the tap on the review page, as before. It resets where the share is in hand, so the way back from the review page shows no stale notice. It has no dismiss: it ends with the review, or at 24 hours. `manifest.spec.ts` has a deep link with a real share in the Cache API. Not tried on a real phone (T-3.7.6.2).

**Open questions (from PR #407, the owner's):**
- OQ-30: should mail stay retrievable in drafts, marked unverified, or be left out entirely? #407 keeps it, because shares reach nothing else and leaving it out gives the mailbox no use beyond the inbox list. A model can still use mail without citing it, which raises no flag. Leaving it out is a small change in `knowledge()`.
- OQ-31: should the owner be able to mark a mail as their own words by hand? It is blocked at the intake, because a From line can be forged. Lifting it means removing `_check_authorship` and the inbox condition.
- OQ-32: mail counts toward readiness domain 5 (the share count and content types) like any share, so a forged stream could fill that count. It does not reach the voice gate, which needs a passed voice test.

### E-3.8 🟢 Always-evokable Echo surface

**Filed 2026-10-18.** The floating bubble on every page: one click or `Ctrl-E` expands a chat panel; below readiness it shows the meter and what is missing; above it, it answers *as the user* by routing to the E-3.4 agent for drafts and to the library / history agents for questions, and surfaces anticipation cards (unread saved items, suggested questions from E-8.4).
**Acceptance.** The bubble is present for Studio users on every authenticated page, opens with its shortcut, shows readiness progress in words, and once ready holds a conversation whose answers cite sources, facts and past Q&As; it never covers the Q&A composer on mobile.

#### S-3.8.1 🟢 Bubble, panel and shortcut
**Shipped:** 2026-10-20 — PR [#355](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/355). Depends on the `useShortcuts` hook from S-8.2.2.
**Calls made.**
1. *No `/echo/chat`.* The panel asks the library and the user's past questions through `POST /library/qa` and `POST /qa-history/chat`. Quota, the Q&A library index, corpus statistics and trust weights then work as on those pages, and each answer is saved where it was asked rather than as a third kind of exchange. The user picks what to ask over (**My library** or **My past questions**) instead of a model classifying intent: one call fewer, and the choice is visible.
2. *Ask is open below readiness.* Readiness gates answering *as* the user, not asking the library. The panel shows readiness in words above both modes.
3. *Draft as me shows its gate.* The panel reads the per-user gate ([D-100](decisions.md#d-100--voice-match-gate-mode-a-passes-on-synthetic-writers-speak-as-me-may-be-built-and-is-enabled-per-user-only-after-the-user-as-judge-test-2026-10-19)) and says what is missing. Until S-3.4.1 ships it, the tab says "Not on this install yet" rather than disappearing. Contract proposed to lane A: `GET /echo/draft/status` → `{enabled, state, reasons, notes, notes_needed}`, and T-3.4.1.2's `POST /echo/draft`. The panel tolerates missing fields.
4. *On phones the opener sits in the top bar,* so it can never cover the docked composer (S-8.12.1). The panel fills the screen there, and focus goes to its close button so the keyboard does not cover it.
5. *Mod-Shift-E,* the plan's default. Firefox binds Ctrl-Shift-E to its Network Monitor, so **Ask Echo** in the palette is the other way in.

The panel is labelled as an AI. On desktop it is non-modal and stays open beside the page; Escape closes it and focus returns to the opener.
- [x] T-3.8.1.1 `EchoBubble` mounted in `AppLayout` behind `has('echo_personal_brain')`; panel with readiness state and chat, labelled as AI (EU AI Act Art. 50(1) applies to a conversational surface from 2026-08-02). Shortcut: **not** `Cmd-E` as the roadmap sketched (it is macOS's system-wide "Use Selection for Find" and clashes in Safari and Chrome); the plan proposes `Ctrl/Cmd-Shift-E` plus an "Ask Echo" entry in the `Ctrl-K` palette
- [x] T-3.8.1.2 *(re-scoped: no `/echo/chat`; the existing Q&A endpoints and S-3.4.1's draft endpoint, see call 1)* `POST /echo/chat` routing by intent to draft, library Q&A or history Q&A; exchanges stored as `qa_history_exchanges` with `source=echo` so they enter the Q&A library index
- [x] T-3.8.1.3 *(in the top bar instead, see call 4)* Mobile placement above the docked composer (S-8.12.1)

#### S-3.8.2 🟢 Anticipation cards
**Shipped:** 2026-10-20 — PR [#358](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/358). **Linked decision:** [D-103](decisions.md#d-103--anticipation-cards-only-in-the-opened-panel-three-a-view-three-days-a-key-three-dismissals-turn-a-kind-off-2026-10-20). The frequency cap and dismissal rule were pre-registered before any card was built ([note](notes/2026-10-20-anticipation-cards-preregistration.md), committed first on the branch).

**Calls made.**
1. *Only in the opened panel,* and only while its conversation is empty. Nothing badges the bubble.
2. *What "shares not yet read" means.* Shares have no read state, so it means direct shares (never imports) that processing finished or failed since the user last opened the inbox. The inbox page reports when it opens (`POST /echo/cards/seen/shares`). With no visit on record, the last 14 days count.
3. *Questions for the page in view* use S-8.4.1's contexts: a run or its report (`job:<id>`), a knowledge page (`document:<id>`), else `library`. A question goes into the panel's Ask field, which asks the library, and records a `fill` with the suggestions feedback.
4. *A card counts as shown only once it renders.* The live check found the questions card empty in a new library, which would have understated its act rate. The note records this addition, dated before the first card shipped.
5. *Card settings live in the panel.* No Echo page edit, since other lanes are working on that page.
- [x] T-3.8.2.1 Cards in the panel: suggested questions for the current page (E-8.4), shares not yet read, saved searches that changed (E-8.3)

### E-3.9 🔴 Mode B: opt-in per-user fine-tuning

**Filed 2026-10-18.** After Mode A is stable and the readiness data shows the corpus warrants it: curate per-theme datasets (problem-solution, recommendation lens, situational priority, opinion-formation, methodology) from the user's signals and exchanges, train an adapter, and route `echo_draft` to it with retrieval still augmenting. Provider and cost model are open questions in the plan.
**Status.** 🔴 deferred (was ⚪ proposed, not scheduled until S-3.3.4 shows Mode A plateauing). **Parked 2026-10-25** ([D-110](decisions.md#d-110--echo-only-sees-what-the-user-hands-it-no-background-capture-and-the-echo-scope-rulings-2026-10-25)): it stays parked until the owner's own voice test, the user-as-judge run (T-3.3.4.3), has run. **Research 2026-10-19:** OpenAI is winding down its self-serve fine-tuning platform (no new jobs after 2027-01-06; the fine-tunable models retire 2027-01-14), Anthropic fine-tuning exists only for Claude 3 Haiku on Bedrock in one region, and the one head-to-head study found direct LoRA style tuning raises style accuracy while collapsing preferred quality. Mode B therefore has to be provider-agnostic from the first line, with local LoRA on open weights as the durable path and a quality guard before any adapter is served.

#### S-3.9.1 ⚪ Theme datasets
- [ ] T-3.9.1.1 `dataset_service` gains per-theme Echo exports built from exchanges, drafts the user accepted and signals with examples, in the OpenAI chat JSONL already shipped (byte-compatible with Hugging Face TRL's `messages` format) plus thin Bedrock-Claude and Vertex variants, every example tagged with `language`, `register`, `source_id` and `consent_version` so themes can be curated and revoked
#### S-3.9.2 ⚪ Training adapter
- [ ] T-3.9.2.1 Provider abstraction: local LoRA on an open-weights model (Apache-2.0 tooling; about 6 GB VRAM floor for an 8B QLoRA, 12 to 16 GB realistic) first, hosted supervised fine-tune where a provider still offers it; job tracking, cost shown beforehand; persona-vector style regression prompts and a preference-rate quality guard before serving
#### S-3.9.3 ⚪ Routing
- [ ] T-3.9.3.1 Per-user override of the `echo_draft` use case to the tuned model through the E-1.13 override mechanism; delete weights on revoke

### E-3.10 🟡 Privacy model: export, delete, zero-knowledge

**Linked decision:** [D-089](decisions.md#d-089--echos-privacy-contract-context-only-by-default-the-brain-is-a-registry-the-archive-is-the-users-whole-copy-delete-in-a-fixed-order-2026-10-19).
**Open question (the user's):** should context-only share text stay in the whole-brain archive (built, D-089 item 3), or move to a separate flagged section? Not omitted either way, or the archive stops being lossless. ✅ **Resolved 2026-10-25 by [D-110](decisions.md#d-110--echo-only-sees-what-the-user-hands-it-no-background-capture-and-the-echo-scope-rulings-2026-10-25):** it stays, as shipped in #286.
**Progress 2026-10-19.** Export and delete shipped with the first new Echo table ([#286](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/286)). **Rule for every later Echo story:** a new Echo table goes into `echo_privacy_service.ECHO_TABLES`, a new Echo Chroma collection into `echo_vectors.echo_collection_names()`, and a new Echo file store under the tenant directory, or the export misses it and the delete leaves it behind. The connector-grant table (E-3.2) is the first credential store this applies to.

**Filed 2026-10-18.** The hard constraint from `personal-brain.md`: self-host data is local; every connector is opt-in, scoped and revocable; the whole brain is exportable and deletable; SaaS offers a zero-knowledge mode.
**Acceptance.** A user downloads one archive containing facts, events, signals, shares and drafts; a full delete leaves no row, file, token or Chroma vector attributed to them; every connector's consent text is shown before activation and stored with the grant.

#### S-3.10.1 🟢 Full export
**PR:** [#286](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/286) — merged 2026-10-19.
- [x] T-3.10.1.1 `GET /echo/export` streaming a zip of JSONL per table plus files; audit event. Built in a temporary file (constant memory) and removed after sending. `manifest.json` carries row counts, every file with size and SHA-256, and any file a row points at that was missing. `echo_privacy_service.read_archive` is the exact inverse, and `test_echo_privacy.py` holds every column of every row and every file's bytes to it. The archive includes context-only share content: it is the user's own copy of what they handed over (Article 20). The audit event carries counts only
#### S-3.10.2 🟢 Full delete
**PR:** [#286](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/286) — merged 2026-10-19.
- [x] T-3.10.2.1 `DELETE /echo/brain?confirm=delete-everything` cascading across every Echo table, file directory, credential and Chroma collection; verified by a test that counts zero rows afterwards. The test looks in each store directly (rows, the tenant directory, both Echo collections) for both tenants: the deleting tenant's footprint is zero and the other tenant's is unchanged. Order is vectors, files, then rows in one commit, so a failing store stops the delete before the rows that would let a retry find what is left (tested). The service re-counts every store afterwards and fails rather than report success with data left. No Echo credential exists yet; the library, notes, account and BYOK keys are outside Echo and untouched. `GET /echo/brain/footprint` shows the same counts
#### S-3.10.3 🔴 Zero-knowledge mode (SaaS)
- [ ] T-3.10.3.1 Design only: client-side encryption of share payloads and signals with a user-held key; the plan records that server-side embedding and extraction cannot run on ciphertext, so this mode disables them. Decision deferred to SaaS launch. *2026-10-25 (D-110): private-by-key encryption (L3 plan question 1) waits until SaaS.*

### E-4.1 🟢 `knowledge-curator` skill

**Scope.** Skill at `.claude/skills/knowledge-curator/SKILL.md` that scans session, routes content into the canonical docs (`feature-roadmap.md`, `architecture.md`, `requirements.md`, `source-types.md`, `branding.md`, `vision.md`, `personal-brain.md`, `saas-roadmap.md`, `decisions.md`), commits on a branch, opens PR.
**Status verified 2026-04-28** — skill has been invoked dozens of times across the multi-session L1 rollout, has correctly opened curator PRs (e.g. PRs #94, #97, #98, #101), correctly no-ops on tactical sessions (PR-merge sessions, status-only sessions), and reliably routes ADRs / source-types / feature-roadmap edits into the right files.

### E-4.2 🟢 `work-tracker` skill

**Scope.** Skill at `.claude/skills/work-tracker/SKILL.md` that owns this file (`docs/initiatives.md`). Updates status / scope of existing items; creates new items for newly-discussed work; cross-links to decisions and PRs; commits on a branch, opens PR.
**Status verified 2026-04-28** — skill has filed S-1.5.11 (D-020), S-1.5.3 / S-1.5.4 framing/polymorphic updates (D-014/016/018), I-2 audit (PR #93), OQ-1 through OQ-11 lifecycle tracking, and the I-4 audit captured in this PR. Sibling-PR pattern with curator works reliably.

### E-4.3 🟢 Stop-hook auto-invocation

**Scope.** `.claude/settings.json` Stop hook nudges Claude once per session (via `stop_hook_active` guard) to invoke both skills before ending. Skills are no-op-safe.
**Status verified 2026-04-28** — Stop hook fired 8+ times across the 2026-04-26 → 2026-04-28 sessions; recursion-guard works; both skills triggered or correctly no-op'd as appropriate. Per-session reliability has been 100%.

**Shipped** in the bootstrap PR. The log has since grown organically to D-022 through curator-driven adds — the seed serves its purpose.

### E-4.6 🟢 Coordinated PR composition (skills share a PR per session)

**Closed 2026-05-04.** The "second skill checks out the first's branch + adds a commit" pattern is now both **documented in `SKILL.md`** (under the "Coordination with /knowledge-curator" section in `work-tracker/SKILL.md`) **and practiced in production** across multiple sessions: combined PRs include [#139](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/139) (D-031/D-032 + T-1.6.6 follow-up), [#143](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/143) (M-1.7/T-1.6.6/M-1.8 reconciliation), [#147](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/147) (I-1 closure).

Sibling-PR fallback is still acceptable when timing or branch-state makes a shared branch awkward — the convention is "shared by default, sibling when easier", with a `Companion PR: #N` cross-reference in either case.

### E-4.7 🟢 Inventions / novel-ideas log

**Closed 2026-05-04.** [`inventions.md`](inventions.md) ships as a canonical doc with the full template + conventions. The `/knowledge-curator` skill includes the detection heuristic for filing `N-NNN` entries.

**Status (2026-05-04 inventory).** Zero `N-NNN` entries filed to date. The project's work has been **engineering with established patterns** — polymorphic dispatch, factory base classes, opt-in extras, two-phase migrations — none of which qualify as novel mechanisms. The infrastructure (template + skill heuristic + doc location) is ready when a genuine novel concept surfaces. False-negative bias is the expensive failure mode per the doc's own conventions, so the bar stays "we'd file even something borderline" — there's just been nothing borderline yet.

**Scope.** Canonical doc [`inventions.md`](inventions.md) owned by `/knowledge-curator`. Captures novel mechanisms / non-obvious combinations / potentially-patentable concepts surfaced in conversation. Skill detection heuristic biased toward over-capture; verbatim user messages flagged as novel are also saved raw to `docs/notes/<YYYY-MM-DD-novel-<slug>.md`. Skill makes no legal patentability assessment.
**Linked decision:** [D-012](decisions.md#d-012--capture-novel--potentially-patentable-ideas-in-inventionsmd-2026-04-25)
**PR:** [#68](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/68) (follow-up commit on the bootstrap branch)

Idempotent — re-running on already-fixed docs is a no-op. **13 residual heading-rename casualties resolved 2026-05-06** in the follow-up branch — every cross-doc anchor now resolves cleanly.

The script rewrote `#d-063--per-user-...` to `#d-063-per-user-...`, but GitHub's rendered HTML (contents API, `html` media type) gives `id="user-content-d-063--per-user-..."`, and `e-510--per-user-isolation-r2` for an emoji heading: every dropped em dash or emoji leaves two hyphens. So the 132 rewrites in PR #179 need re-checking against rendered anchors, and the script's oracle should be GitHub's output rather than its own slug function. The script now scans every Markdown file and every link form (same-file, relative, reference), resolves a path from the linking file (which exposed three real 404s in CHANGELOG that the old stem match passed), numbers repeated headings across ATX, setext and blockquoted forms, and ignores code and front matter. It is a dry run by default, with `--check` (exit 1 on a bad link) and `--write`; `--report` is gone. It says what it does not read: raw HTML headings and a heading inside a list item. `heading_slugs` was compared with GitHub's rendered ids on every doc: 70 files, 2,021 ids, no difference. ### E-4.10 🟢 Replace `Base.metadata.create_all` lifespan with Alembic-managed schema

**Closed 2026-05-06** in [PR #179](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/179). New `app/services/schema_init_service.py` with `ensure_schema_at_head(db_url, orm_metadata)` handles four cases cleanly:
- `fresh_install` — empty DB → `alembic upgrade head`.
- `already_at_head` — no-op.
- `upgraded` — DB at intermediate revision → normal upgrade.
- `stamped_recovery` — tables exist (via pre-E-4.10 `create_all`) but `alembic_version` is stale → upgrade fails with "already exists" → service detects schema-matches-ORM bidirectionally → auto-stamp head. **Closes the operator upgrade-path bug** for anyone who installed before this session's migrations.

Strict bidirectional schema-match check (every ORM table in DB AND every DB table in ORM) prevents silent papering-over of real drift; mismatches raise with a clear "manual operator intervention required" message pointing at `alembic stamp <revision>`.

**Tasks**
- [x] T-4.10.1 Remove `Base.metadata.create_all` from lifespan — *shipped*.
- [x] T-4.10.2 Replace with `ensure_schema_at_head` — *shipped*.
- [x] T-4.10.3 Recovery path is the auto-stamp branch above; no separate runbook needed since it's automatic + observable via the lifespan log line. Operators with drifted schemas hit the manual-intervention error message which carries the exact `stamp` command — *shipped*.
- [x] T-4.10.4 conftest.py keeps using `Base.metadata.create_all` directly against in-memory SQLite for tests (independent of production lifespan; intentional) — *unchanged*.
- [x] T-4.10.5 End-to-end test in `tests/test_services/test_schema_init.py::test_real_alembic_recovers_from_create_all_conflict` reproduces the conflict + verifies recovery — *shipped*.
- [x] T-4.10.6 🟢 **Column-drift detection + auto-recovery shipped 2026-05-06** in this branch's follow-up. `_attempt_recovery` now checks bidirectional **table** match → detects **column** drift via `_column_drift` (compares live `inspect(eng).get_columns(t)` against `orm_metadata.tables[t].columns`) → if drift is purely additive (`missing_in_db` only), runs `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` for each missing column then stamps head; refuses with a clear "non-additive drift" error if the DB has columns the ORM doesn't (destructive direction is a manual operator call). 3 new tests cover (a) additive recovery, (b) destructive-drift refusal, (c) end-to-end against the real ORM with `users.failed_login_attempts` / `users.locked_until` reconstructed. Operators no longer need to `rm data/<db>` after pulling new migrations against pre-E-4.10 schemas.

- [x] T-4.10.7 🟢 **In-process migrations were silencing all application logging — fixed 2026-07-31** ([PR #219](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/219)). Moving schema init *into the process* (the whole point of this epic) had a side effect nobody looked for: `alembic/env.py` calls `fileConfig(...)`, whose `disable_existing_loggers` defaults to `True`, setting `.disabled` on every logger not named in `alembic.ini` — the whole `app.*` tree. On a fresh install the app lost its entire log for the process lifetime, starting with the `logger.info(f"schema_init: {result}")` on the next line. Standalone `alembic upgrade` was unaffected, which is why it survived from 2026-05-06 to 2026-07-31. Fixed with `disable_existing_loggers=False`; pinned by `test_startup_migration_does_not_disable_app_loggers`, which asserts the migration actually ran so it cannot pass vacuously. Surfaced from an unrelated flaky R1 test — see [docs/testing.md](testing.md) §3.5.

13 schema_init tests; all paths covered. End-to-end against the project's real ORM + migrations. Live boot post-fix: `lifespan: schema-init result=stamped_recovery` (when applicable) followed by clean `/api/v1/health` `ok`.

### E-4.9 🟢 LLM smoke-probe `gpt-5.4` config audit

**Closed 2026-05-06** in [PR #179](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/179). Audit found `gpt-5.4` and `gpt-5.4-mini` (the registry's defaults) are NOT in the configured OpenAI account's available models — the available `gpt-5.x` family is `gpt-5.4-nano`, `gpt-5.4-pro`, `gpt-5.5`, `gpt-5.5-pro`. Updated `USE_CASE_REGISTRY` (`app/services/llm_routing.py`):
- `gpt-5.4-mini` → `gpt-5.4-nano` (small / fast tier)
- `gpt-5.4` → `gpt-5.5` (flagship tier)

Verified post-fix boot: `/api/v1/health` reports `llm.status: ok`, `unavailable_features: []`. All 3 unique configs (`openai:gpt-5.4-nano:off`, `openai:gpt-5.5:low`, `openai:gpt-4.1-mini:off`) probe successfully.

- [x] T-4.9.2 Registry rewrite — *shipped*.

New `backend/scripts/validate_llm_registry.py` walks unique `(provider, model)` pairs in `USE_CASE_REGISTRY` (post-`resolve_config`, so env overrides apply), calls `client.models.list()` per provider, and reports drift. `--strict` exits 1 on any miss for CI use. Suggests near-matches when a model isn't found. OpenAI is the only provider exercised today; Anthropic / Google get a `?` line + skipped count. Verified live: 3 unique pairs all `OK`. Schedule manually or via Windows Task Scheduler.
- [x] T-4.9.5 🟢 **Probe budget fix shipped 2026-05-06** in this branch's follow-up. Picked option (a) — `probe_config()` now uses `max_tokens=256` when `cfg.reasoning != "off"`, keeps the minimal 16-token budget for non-reasoning configs. Reasoning models burn budget on internal thinking before any visible output, so 256 covers the typical `low` / `medium` budget overhead while still being cheap. Verified live: `/api/v1/health` returns `llm.status: ok`, `unavailable: []` against `gpt-5.5:low`.

#### S-4.11.2 🟢 Gaps the docs audit documented and did not fix
**Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#414](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/414), PR [#419](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/419) and PR [#420](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/420) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04), each task below naming its own.

  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#420](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/420) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-143](decisions.md#d-143--the-apis-content-security-policy-is-locked-by-default-oauth-redirects-are-compared-as-origins-a-socket-ends-at-its-tokens-expiry-and-a-shared-link-is-logged-by-host-alone-2026-11-05). `app/middleware/content_security_policy.py`, a pure ASGI middleware, outermost, so a 429 and a preflight carry it. JSON gets `default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'`, and anything else nobody named also gets `sandbox`, so a new route starts locked. The stored report keeps its inline styles and runs nothing. `/docs` and `/redoc` name their CDN files, and Swagger's inline script is allowed by a hash of the page as sent. Checked by hand in Chromium and Edge. The README gives the policy for the app itself, and `docs/requirements.md` is corrected. Starlette's last-resort 500 carries none. **Owner step:** a production host serving the built app should send the README's policy.

It is applied from `app/__init__.py`, again after Alembic's `fileConfig`, and through Celery's logger signals. Under `json`, Python warnings go through `captureWarnings`, and each place that builds a model first calls `route_model_libraries`, which sends `transformers` and `huggingface_hub` through the root logger: a real load wrote ten bare lines before it. Under `json`, Celery's banner and the `JWT_SECRET is blank` warning stay text (T-4.11.3.2).

Anything else is a 400 with nothing stored, and what passes reaches the provider as sent. The Echo connector authorize route takes no redirect, so it needs no list. `localhost` and `127.0.0.1` stay different origins. **Owner step:** an app opened at any other address needs `APP_BASE_URL` set to it or the origin added, or Google and GitHub sign-in answer 400.

- [x] T-4.11.4.1 🟢 Under uvicorn's default logging the root logger has no handler, so in the API every `app.*` INFO record was dropped and a WARNING went out bare through `logging.lastResort`, with no time, level or logger. Only a process that had just migrated in-process at startup wrote them, through Alembic's handler
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#437](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/437) (batch 9 of 2026-11-09). Linked decision: [D-157](decisions.md#d-157--the-apps-log-lines-get-a-handler-that-steps-aside-while-the-root-logger-has-one-an-in-process-migration-restores-the-root-logger-and-a-log-line-names-no-email-recipient-subject-or-connector-account-2026-11-09). A new `AppStderrHandler` in `app/utils/json_logging.py` sits on the `app` logger, never on root, and `configure_logging` adds it under `text` (and under an unknown value, which reads as text). It writes to the current `sys.stderr` at INFO, as `time LEVEL logger: message`, and only while the root logger has no handler, checked for each record. Wherever something else gives root a handler (the Celery worker and beat, Alembic's `fileConfig`, `LOG_FORMAT=json`, pytest, a script's `basicConfig`), each line is still written once, in that handler's format. It is found by name, so a second `configure_logging` or a reloaded module adds no copy. Root's handlers and level are untouched, so httpx's whole URLs, chromadb and sentence-transformers stay as quiet as before. `schema_init_service._keeping_the_root_logger` puts the root logger back after an in-process `alembic upgrade` or `stamp`; before, Alembic's handler kept root for the rest of the process and every app line lost its time. **Audit:** all 254 `logger.info` and `logger.debug` calls under `app/` were read, and three lines in code the API runs were changed at the call site: `chroma_service.query_collection` logs the question's length, not its first 80 characters; `email_service.send_email`'s SMTP success line names neither recipient nor subject; and the Echo connector's connect line names the connector, not the account label. **Found on the way:** with `SMTP_HOST` unset, the password-reset email, logged at INFO, was dropped too, so CLAUDE.md's documented hand-over never worked in an ordinary API start. It now reaches the API's stderr, tested through the real route. **Visible change in the worker:** warnings logged before Celery sets up logging, such as the held `JWT_SECRET` warning, now carry time, level and logger. **Review fix:** the subprocess test sets the install's secrets to empty strings rather than removing them, so `backend/.env` cannot supply an SMTP host and send a real email. A live check ran the real uvicorn CLI on a spare port with every use case routed to a loopback server: the X-token line, `llm_smoke`'s lines and the startup line reached stderr with their time. CLAUDE.md's `LOG_FORMAT` and `SMTP_HOST` rows, `.env.example` and the comments in `config.py` and `main.py` are corrected. *2026-11-09 (batch 9's live check, after the restart): `.uvicorn.err.log` holds the app's own lines with time, level and logger, 21 `app.*` INFO lines at startup with T-1.5.9.1's X-token line among them. No JWT or provider-key shape is in the new logs, the startup lines carry ids and counts and no user's words, and the worker and beat logs double no line (the one repeated line is the Hugging Face Hub's own warning, printed by two model loads).* *2026-11-09 (batch 10's live check, after the restart): `.uvicorn.err.log` writes each of the app's lines once, with no doubled line.*
- [x] T-4.11.4.2 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-09, from #437)* Worker-only INFO lines that carry a user's words: a topic job's `topic=`, `search_videos`' planner queries, the Mastodon search query, and channel inputs, which can be a pasted URL. The worker already wrote them, so #437 exposed nothing new; a pass like #437's audit would change them at the call site
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#442](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/442) (batch 10 of 2026-11-09). Linked decision: [D-160](decisions.md#d-160--a-users-words-leave-the-workers-log-at-the-call-site-an-exception-that-can-quote-a-request-is-logged-by-its-frames-and-a-connectors-search-counts-as-a-shared-link-2026-11-09). Wider than the four lines named: every log call that interpolates a value under `app/tasks`, `app/agents`, `app/sources`, `app/services`, `app/connectors` and `app/utils` was read, 566 of them at every level, and 39 lines changed in code the worker runs; the PR lists each, and why the rest stay. Two helpers in `app/utils/shared_url_log.py`: `traceback_without_text(exc)` keeps an exception's frames and types and drops every message, and goes to `logger.error` where `logger.exception` would write an `HttpError`'s, httpx's or a database error's text; `loggable_channel_input(text)` names a channel input by its kind, an id or `@handle` as written, a link by its host and a name by its length. A topic, a planner query or a hashtag is logged by its length or its place, and a failed YouTube request by its status and Google's reason code (`_http_error_label`). The eight connector search methods (HN, Reddit twice, Bluesky, Mastodon, X, Brave and iTunes) and Echo's GitHub `_default_request` send inside `shared_url_fetch()`, so httpx writes no line with their query. `errors_by_source_type` holds the type alone, a GitHub error names the endpoint, not the login or repository, and the SMTP failure line names no recipient or subject. `DEBUG`'s SQL echo writes bound values, checked on SQLAlchemy 2.0.48, and CLAUDE.md's `DEBUG` row and `.env.example` now say so. **Review fixes:** an input counts as a channel id only by its exact shape (`is_channel_id`), so "UC Berkeley Data Science" is no longer logged and returned as an id; `social_classify`'s three fallback lines log a length, a JSON type or the failing field names; and `hide_parameters=not settings.DEBUG`, first in the PR, was taken out because the brief kept `DEBUG`'s behaviour (OQ-45). Tested end to end through `POST /jobs` and the real `execute_topic_job`, search agent, `youtube_service` and HN, Mastodon and Bluesky clients, with only the network faked and Celery's formatter on the root logger; an in-suite mutation shows the check sees a leak, and each call site has a patched-logger test. API-only lines are T-4.11.4.4. **Owner step:** older `.celery.*.log` files still hold what was written before. *2026-11-09 (batch 10's live check, after the restart): the worker's lines carry ids and counts and no user's words. The only SQL echo is the owner's `DEBUG=true`, and at idle its parameters hold no user's words.*

Not a secret, and CI and worktrees have no `.env`
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#443](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/443) (batch 10 of 2026-11-09). No decision needed. The helpers that build a logging test's subprocess environment now set each setting that shapes what the test reads instead of removing it, since a set variable beats `backend/.env`. `test_json_logging.py`'s `_env` sets `LOG_FORMAT` to the field default when a test passes `None`, `DEBUG=false` and `JWT_SECRET`; `test_log_redaction.py`'s sets the provider keys to empty strings, `LOG_FORMAT` and `DEBUG=false`. The default, not an empty string, because `""` logs an unknown-value warning that the text tests would read. A new `tests/dotenv_settings.py` loads the real `Settings` under a helper's environment with a `.env` the test writes, never the real one; eight new cases use it, and six mutation checks fail them. With `LOG_FORMAT=json`, `DEBUG=true` and a blank `JWT_SECRET` in a temporary `.env` in the worktree, all 23 subprocess tests pass. **Also fixed:** #442's `test_handle_failures_fallback_names_neither_the_error_nor_its_values` failed on master once #440 merged, because #440 moved `_handle_failure`'s write to `_write_unless_stopped`; the test now breaks that write. Test only.
- [x] T-4.11.4.4 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-09, from #442)* A WARNING and ERROR pass over the API's own code, which T-4.11.4.2 left out as API-only: `echo_import_service.preview` and `connectors/registry` log an uploaded export's file name when an importer fails, `auth_service.warn_unregistered_operators` logs the listed operator addresses at startup, and `echo_agent`'s classify and critique failures log the exception
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#447](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/447) (batch 11 of 2026-11-09). Linked decision: [D-164](decisions.md#d-164--the-apis-warnings-and-errors-carry-no-users-words-an-operator-by-place-and-mask-echos-model-failures-by-type-and-status-a-chosen-file-name-by-its-extension-and-length-and-a-failure-logged-by-its-frames-is-never-re-raised-bare-2026-11-09). Every warning, error, exception and critical call in code the API runs was read: `app/main.py`, the routers, the middleware and the websocket manager, and the services, agents and connectors a request calls, including the Echo paths under the `inline` modes, `POST /author/outputs?sync=true`, the PDF upload and the pasted-URL ingest, 265 calls in 83 files. 21 lines changed at the call site; the PR lists each, and why the rest stay. Two helpers in `app/utils/shared_url_log.py`: `loggable_filename` names a file name a user chose by its extension and length, and `exception_kind` an exception by its type and any HTTP status. `auth_service.warn_unregistered_operators` names an `ADMIN_EMAILS` entry by its place and a mask (`entry 2 of 3 (b***@e***.com)`). An import's file name goes by `loggable_filename` in `echo_import_service` and `connectors/registry`. Echo's classify, critique, signals-batch and voice-test retry lines use `exception_kind`. `verify_password`, registration's `IntegrityError`, the lifespan's `ADMIN_EMAILS` check, `audit_service.record`, `maybe_queue`, the voice test's generate, `process_share` and its event, the connector sync crash, `output_service`'s generation and `/qa/stream` log by `traceback_without_text` at error. A fabricated citation's title and channel, and a non-JSON knowledge reply, go by their lengths. `run_import` raises `ImportStopped`, and `POST /echo/import` answers 500 with the row's words instead of letting the failure reach uvicorn's unhandled-exception line. **Review fixes:** a failed preview ran outside every handler and reached uvicorn's line quoting the cell it could not read; it now raises `ImportStopped("Preview stopped: <type>")`, answered as a 500 by both import routes, the import before any row is written, while `ExportUnreadable` keeps its 422. An operator's place counts the list as written, so a repeated address no longer shifts it. Tested by a patched logger at each changed call and end to end through `POST /echo/import/preview`, `POST /echo/import` and `POST /echo/draft` with a root handler that renders tracebacks; in-suite mutations show the checks see a leak. CLAUDE.md's `ADMIN_EMAILS` row, `.env.example` and `docs/api-reference.md` are updated. Left out: uvicorn's access log (T-4.11.4.5), an exception no route handles (T-4.11.4.6) and two worker-only sites (T-4.11.4.7). **Owner step:** restart the API and the worker.

  **Shipped:** 2026-11-10 — PR [#454](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/454) (batch 12 of 2026-11-10).
