The three readers are `React.lazy` routes inside `Suspense`, with one loading line (`ReaderLoading`) for the chunk and the data. DOMPurify, html-react-parser and remark-gfm left the first paint for a shared `reader` chunk (42.58 kB gzip): the main chunk went from 290.00 to 241.82 kB gzip, and first paint from 349.60 to 307.81 kB. **Not met:** the unified stack stayed in the main chunk, and the 170 kB budget is still missed. That remainder is T-8.8.4.5, so the routes are ticked here and the budget is open there
- [x] T-8.8.4.2 Make the book outputter's table-of-contents anchors match the heading ids it writes: `book_markdown._slugify(title)` gives `#chapter-title` for a heading that reads `## 1. Chapter title`, so every TOC link was dead in the old Author modal. The reader repoints them (D-086); any other consumer of the manuscript (the S-6.1.5 PDF / EPUB export, the site outputter) will not
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#410](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/410) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-136](decisions.md#d-136--a-books-contents-take-their-ids-from-the-rendered-manuscript-the-main-chunk-meets-its-budget-with-lazy-markdown-and-lazy-routes-and-the-device-suites-server-is-dual-stack-2026-11-04). The bug as filed was already fixed by T-6.1.4.4, and `_slugify` was dead code, now removed. Real rendered ids exposed three that remained: a chapter's headings were invisible to the id scan, so the book's Conclusion linked to a report's; typed titles such as `a ] b` broke links; and the reader did not slug the book title. Contents ids now come from rendering the manuscript with `book_html`, keyed by source line, and are tested in Markdown, EPUB, PDF and the reader. Typed titles are written as literal Markdown. The EPUB knows the book's contents and endnotes by text and place (the first and last `h2`), and an answer's headings sit below the chapter's, so a stored answer cannot add a chapter. The site outputter has no contents section. Stored books keep their old contents until regenerated, and the reader's repointing stays for them.
- [x] T-8.8.4.3 Run the frontend suites in CI: `npm test` (vitest) and `npm run test:mobile` (Playwright, needs `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium webkit` in the runner). Today CI runs pytest, ruff and the frontend build only ([testing.md §5.4](testing.md#54-ci))
  **Shipped:** 2026-10-25 — PR [#375](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/375). The premise was wrong: no GitHub Actions workflow had ever existed, although `testing.md` §5.4 said CI blocked failing PRs. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` now runs five gating jobs on pull requests and pushes to `master`, none `continue-on-error` and none needing a secret: backend pytest (Python 3.12, CPU PyTorch, `-rs`, the embedder and Chroma's ONNX model cached under `MODEL_CACHE_KEY`, saved only after a successful download), ruff on the bug-finding rules `E9,F63,F7,F82`, the frontend build plus vitest, the extension's `node --test`, and Playwright device tests on `windows-latest` (the baselines are Windows renders). A pull request's newer push cancels its older run; every master push runs to completion. A fresh install found real breaks, fixed in the same PR: `bcrypt>=4.0.1,<5.0.0` (chromadb needs 4, bcrypt 5 breaks passlib 1.7.4), `iter_knowledge_rows` on SQLAlchemy 2.1, `test_cancel_job` revoking through a real Redis, and the device suite, red since #328 (21 of 41 failed: nothing answered `GET /notifications` or the reader's `GET /videos/{id}/frames`). `tests/test_ci/test_ci_workflow.py` pins the concurrency and cache rules. Not done: requiring the checks before a merge is a branch-protection setting the owner turns on. Found 2026-10-25 by PR [#380](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/380): the dev venv runs FastAPI 0.135.2 while CI installs 0.162, which keeps each `include_router` as one `app.routes` entry, so anything that walks `app.routes` sees an empty table on 0.162 (only `test_url_tokens.py` does today, and it handles both)
- [x] T-8.8.4.4 Report the compose step's truncated section ([S-1.14.17](#e-114--model-tier-quality-evaluation-harness)) in the reader when it happens again: the reader cannot repair it, but it could show the processing note instead of silently losing two headings. Only after S-1.14.17 decides how the compose step discloses it
  **Shipped:** 2026-10-25 — PR [#379](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/379), with S-1.14.17. `parseReportHtml` lifts the note out of the body, by its `processing-note` class or, in older reports, by its "Processing note:" label, and `ReportReaderPage` shows it under the statistics tiles as `<aside role="note">`, not repeated in the body. Before, the sanitiser stripped its styling and it sat unnoticed after the statistics. Tests: `parseReport.test.tsx`, `reportReaderNote.test.tsx`
- [x] T-8.8.4.5 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-04, from #396)* Lazy-load the Markdown renderer, the rest of T-8.8.4.1's goal. react-markdown's core (unified, remark-parse, remark-rehype, hast-util-to-jsx-runtime) stays in the main chunk because `JobDetailPage`, `QAHistoryChatPage`, `VideoKnowledgePage`, the notes components, `LibraryQAExchangeCard` and `EchoPanel` import it eagerly. The main chunk is 241.82 kB gzip against the 170 kB budget
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#410](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/410) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-136](decisions.md#d-136--a-books-contents-take-their-ids-from-the-rendered-manuscript-the-main-chunk-meets-its-budget-with-lazy-markdown-and-lazy-routes-and-the-device-suites-server-is-dual-stack-2026-11-04). Pages import `components/common/Markdown`, which loads `react-markdown` on demand and shows the text first in the same elements, with bold and inline code kept, so nothing moves when the real render arrives. A mark comes off only as half of a pair, so the fallback never changes the words, and if the chunk cannot load the text stays. Lazy Markdown alone stopped at 211.51 kB, so seven occasional pages became lazy routes: Echo, its inbox and import, Author, subscription, AI models and system status. Main chunk 246.49 (master had grown past the 241.82 here) to 162.88 kB gzip, under the 170 kB budget, and first paint about 317 to about 238 kB. Device baselines pass unchanged, and a test refuses a static `react-markdown` import in a page. Moving between Echo or Author pages shows a brief spinner the first time in a session.
- [x] T-8.8.4.6 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-04, from #396)* A device-suite flake not on the known list: `[narrow-320] job Q&A thread (light)` failed once in CI with `net::ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED` for Vite's own `react_jsx-dev-runtime.js`, so the app never booted. It passed 30 of 30 repeats locally and on the next CI run. The cause of the refused connection is not established
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#410](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/410) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-136](decisions.md#d-136--a-books-contents-take-their-ids-from-the-rendered-manuscript-the-main-chunk-meets-its-budget-with-lazy-markdown-and-lazy-routes-and-the-device-suites-server-is-dual-stack-2026-11-04). Cause found: a Chromium NetLog shows Windows now and then failing a connect to `[::1]` outright (`WSAEADDRINUSE`, or `WSAENOBUFS`), then Chromium falling back to `127.0.0.1`, where nothing listened. Fixed with `--host ::` on the device suite's dev server. Server on `::1` only: 6 failures in 6,514 pages. Dual-stack: 0 in 5,899, with failed connects rescued in the NetLog. No Playwright retries were added. `--host ::` listens beyond loopback for the length of a device run, so a first local run may show a Windows Firewall prompt, and denying it changes nothing for the tests.
- [x] T-8.8.4.7 🟢 *(proposed 2026-11-04, from #398)* The backend suite reaches huggingface.co when the embedding model loads, so a DNS drop mid-run fails those tests (9 failures in one #398 run, in `test_code_mixing_rendering.py` and `test_echo_personal_vectors.py`). `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` might avoid it; not tried
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#410](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/410) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-136](decisions.md#d-136--a-books-contents-take-their-ids-from-the-rendered-manuscript-the-main-chunk-meets-its-budget-with-lazy-markdown-and-lazy-routes-and-the-device-suites-server-is-dual-stack-2026-11-04). `tests/hf_offline.py` sets `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` from conftest before the hub is imported, only when the model is fully cached, so a first run still downloads. An explicit value is left alone. CI sets it on the pytest step only. With DNS to the hub failing, the two files on this entry went from 10 failed and 136 lookups to all 15 passing with none.

#### S-8.8.5 🟢 Report Markdown download, an output's Sources list, lazy-route errors, two unused routes removed
**Filed and shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#396](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/396) (batch 4 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-123](decisions.md#d-123--unused-read-routes-are-removed-an-outputs-sources-list-groups-by-source-and-a-language-badge-names-a-language-only-on-evidence-2026-11-04). The status plan's API-only gaps, which had no board entry. No migration, no new setting, no data written.
- [x] T-8.8.5.1 **Download Markdown** on the report reader. `GET /jobs/{id}/report.md` (S-6.1.2) already served it, so the button fetches it through the axios client with the bearer header and saves from a blob: URL as `<title-slug>.md`. No token goes in a URL
- [x] T-8.8.5.2 A **Sources** list on the output reader (`components/author/OutputSources.tsx`) from `GET /author/outputs/{id}/citations` (S-6.7.3), which had no UI: one entry per cited source in first-cited order, each citation a chip with its timestamp and link. It is requested only when `citation_count > 0`. Only a book's chips name an endnote number. A book and a deck keep the list off paper because their text lists every cited moment; a site and a newsletter print it. An unmatched bare anchor comes last with no link
- [x] T-8.8.5.3 Every lazy route, `GraphPage` included, has an `errorElement` (`RouteLoadError`: "This page could not be loaded" with **Reload**, inside the app layout), a review fix: `React.lazy` caches a rejected import, and a restart or redeploy can replace chunk hashes
- [x] T-8.8.5.4 Removed `GET /channels/{id}/videos` and `GET /jobs/{id}/shelves`, with `shelf_service.shelves_for_job`. Neither had a caller: the channel page reads `GET /library/videos?channel_id=`, which also carries `job_count` / `job_titles` and the library's filters, and the job page reads shelves from the `job_ids` in the cached `GET /shelves` listing. Tenant-scoping tests now run against those reads, and `test_unused_routes_removed.py` pins each route's absence
- Tests: vitest `reportReaderMarkdown`, `outputReaderSources`, `lazyReaders` and `lazyRouteFailure` at the production routes, mutation-checked. Six report reader device baselines were re-recorded, because the new button wraps the toolbar on phones. The device suite does not visit the output reader or the library, so the Sources list was seen only in a throwaway render, before the review fixes. The `CLAUDE.md` line the PR left as an owner step was removed by #401. `docs/plans/*` and `docs/feature-roadmap.md` still mention `GET /jobs/{id}/shelves` as planned; they are historical plans.

- [x] T-8.8.6.1 🟢 `tiktoken` downloads its encoding from the network in CI with no cache: the same kind of risk as T-8.8.4.7, not seen failing yet
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#421](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/421) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-147](decisions.md#d-147--the-rendering-parser-decides-what-is-a-heading-in-book-markdown-a-rewrite-parses-what-it-will-emit-and-the-suites-tiktoken-file-lives-at-a-fixed-path-2026-11-05). `backend/tests/tiktoken_cache.py`, run from conftest, keeps `cl100k_base` in `~/.cache/tiktoken`, on a machine and in CI. CI caches that folder and fills it in the existing download step, and `MODEL_CACHE_KEY` goes to `v3`. A copy already in the temp folder is copied over, and any value in `TIKTOKEN_CACHE_DIR` or `DATA_GYM_CACHE_DIR` is left alone. tiktoken has no offline switch, so a test takes its place: it reloads the encoding with requests and DNS failing, and on a runner it fails rather than skips when the file did not come from the cache. Both CI paths ran on a runner, a miss and then a hit.
- [x] T-8.8.6.2 🟢 Setext headings in an answer or in cohesion prose are not demoted, as on master. The EPUB keeps their text, but such a heading still starts a new EPUB file, and one named Endnotes that is the book's last `h2` is still marked as backmatter
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#421](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/421) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-147](decisions.md#d-147--the-rendering-parser-decides-what-is-a-heading-in-book-markdown-a-rewrite-parses-what-it-will-emit-and-the-suites-tiktoken-file-lives-at-a-fixed-path-2026-11-05). `_demote_headings` asks the parser that renders the book, through the new `book_html.top_level_headings`, so `Text` over `---` is demoted like `## Text` and comes out as ATX. Headings in a quote or list keep their level, because the EPUB reads only the top level. Found along the way: cohesion prose did make real `h2`s, through a bare `##`, bare `\r` line endings and marks behind a quote, an indent or a control character; a multi-line question had the same effect, so a question now stays on one line; `str.splitlines` disagreed with the parser; and a rewrite has to parse the text it will emit. Stored books keep their text until regenerated.

A production change, so not part of the test-infra pass *Taken into batch 7 by the main session, because it has no product trade-off; the owner may reverse it.*
  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#427](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/427) (batch 7 of 2026-11-05). Linked decision: [D-152](decisions.md#d-152--cors-reads-the-oauth-origin-list-and-the-app-keeps-tiktokens-encoding-in-a-folder-of-its-own-2026-11-09). New setting `TIKTOKEN_ENCODINGS_DIR`, default `./data/tiktoken`. `app/utils/tiktoken_cache.use_app_folder` sets `TIKTOKEN_CACHE_DIR` at import, in `app/tasks/celery_app.py` for the worker and `app/main.py` for the API. A `TIKTOKEN_CACHE_DIR` or `DATA_GYM_CACHE_DIR` already set wins, so the suite's arrangement from #421 is untouched, and an empty setting means tiktoken's temp default. A copy in the temp folder is copied over atomically, so an install that has run before needs no download. A folder that cannot be made or written gives a warning and leaves the default. **From the review:** with the folder named, tiktoken re-raises a failed write of what it downloaded, and on Windows two processes downloading at once collide on the rename, so every load in `app/` goes through `tiktoken_cache.encoding()`, which removes its own spare download and loads once more; a test keeps any other file from calling `tiktoken.get_encoding`. `chunking._count_tokens` still has no fallback. **Owner step:** restart the API and the worker.

  **Shipped:** 2026-11-09 — PR [#432](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/432) (batch 8 of 2026-11-09). It extends [D-152](decisions.md#d-152--cors-reads-the-oauth-origin-list-and-the-app-keeps-tiktokens-encoding-in-a-folder-of-its-own-2026-11-09) point 5. The nine scripts in `backend/scripts` that count tokens, through `chunk_transcript`, the report or knowledge agent or era extraction, call `tiktoken_cache.use_app_folder(settings.TIKTOKEN_ENCODINGS_DIR)` next to their app imports, after any `.env` loading. The premise was partly wrong: three (`eval_code_mixed_retrieval_heldout`, `eval_code_mixing_e2e`, `measure_code_mixed_retrieval`) used the temp default, while six, `regenerate_report` among them, already had the app's folder because they import `app.tasks.job_tasks` first. Their new line keeps that from resting on an import chain. One test runs `regenerate_report.main()` offline with the encoding only in the app's folder, and another reads every script's syntax tree and pins the nine. The env table, `.env.example`, `config.py` and `docs/testing.md` name the scripts.

With the encoding kept and the two-process race handled they should fire less. Keep them, or fail loudly as chunking does

### E-8.9 🟢 Citation graph and knowledge map (M9)

**Status 2026-10-19.** S-8.9.1 and S-8.9.2 shipped (PRs [#297](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/297), [#301](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/301)); the epic stayed 🟡 for S-8.9.3, which its plan gates on topic edges proving too sparse on a real library.

**Scope-changed 2026-10-25:** 🟡 → 🟢. S-8.9.3 is parked ([D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25)), so only parked work remains. The parked story stays under the shipped epic, as E-5.5 keeps its deferred tasks.

**Scope.** A library-overview graph: documents, channels, Q&A exchanges and knowledge topics as nodes; citations, authorship and shared topics as edges; tenant-scoped; filterable by shelf, channel or job; a "local graph" around any node. Useful rather than a hairball: size caps, degree-based sizing, clustering by channel or topic, and a focused mode by default.
**Plan:** [`docs/plans/m9-citation-graph.md`](plans/m9-citation-graph.md)
**Linked decision:** [D-092](decisions.md#d-092--library-graph-computed-per-request-sigma-used-directly-as-a-lazy-chunk-three-group-colours-the-accessibility-layer-inside-the-story-2026-10-19) (ratified 2026-10-25 by [D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25))
**Acceptance.** `/library/graph` renders the user's library in under three seconds at 2,000 nodes; clicking a document shows its neighbours and a detail card; filtering by shelf narrows the graph; nothing from another tenant appears; the graph degrades to a list on narrow screens.

#### S-8.9.1 🟢 Graph endpoint
**PR:** [#297](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/297) (merged 2026-10-19)
- [x] T-8.9.1.1 `GET /api/v1/library/graph?focus=&depth=&shelf_id=&job_id=&channel_id=&types=&limit=` → `{nodes, edges, truncated, total, shown, summary, focus}`. Nodes: visible documents, their channels (channels with no visible document would be isolated, so they are left out), the tenant's job and library exchanges that cite a visible document (references matched by video id or URL), and topics from knowledge artifacts on at least two visible documents. Edges `published_by`, `about`, `cites`, and derived `co_occurs` (topics sharing at least two documents, five strongest per topic). A foreign shelf or job is a 404
- [x] T-8.9.1.2 Degree then recency pick nodes under the cap (focus first); ids are stable (`document:`, `channel:`, `topic:<lowercased>`, `exchange:<kind>:`); a one-paragraph `summary` (counts, "N of M shown", connected groups and the largest group's main topics) as the textual alternative. Computed per request: about 0.07 s at 2,000 documents and 500 answers, so no edge table
- [x] T-8.9.1.3 Tests: two-reader isolation (nothing of theirs in the payload, their topic absent, my answer citing only their document has no node), an empty visible set is an empty graph, cap and truncation with degree order, focus at depth 1 and 2, channel / job / shelf filters (foreign → 404, empty shelf → empty), types filter, job exchanges
- Found on the way: a topic label's display spelling depended on row order when casings tied; both this and the channel aggregate now break ties toward the capitalised spelling

#### S-8.9.2 🟢 Graph UI
**PR:** [#301](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/301) (merged 2026-10-19)
**Scope-changed 2026-10-19:** sigma is used directly, without `@react-sigma/core` (one less dependency; the canvas needs sigma's camera and coordinate calls anyway). Plan open questions taken at their defaults: sigma over `react-force-graph-2d`; the global view ships, capped at 300 nodes with single-link nodes hidden, and the local view comes from any node, channel or run scope.
- [x] T-8.9.2.1 Lazy route `/library/graph` (`pages/GraphPage.tsx` via `React.lazy`) on `sigma` 4 + `graphology` + `graphology-layout-forceatlas2` + `graphology-communities-louvain`: its own chunk, 125 kB gzipped, nothing in the main bundle. ForceAtlas2 runs in its web worker and stops once settled (1.2–4 s by size). Louvain groups (seeded, so stable) colour the three largest groups; the rest are grey. A glyph marks each kind (`▸` channel, `#` topic, `?` question). Hover labels, click → detail card (source, channel page, the answer on its run or library page, "Centre the map here"), double-click re-centres, a selection dims everything but its neighbourhood
- [x] T-8.9.2.2 Filters in one row: Map / Table, kinds (channels, topics, your questions), shelf, single-link nodes (with the hidden count); scope chips for the centred node (with a 1–3 step depth slider), channel and run. All of it lives in the URL (`focus`, `depth`, `shelf_id`, `channel_id`, `job_id`, `types`, `leaves`, `view`), and a new centre is a Back step. A legend names each coloured group with its main topics. Reduced motion computes the layout before the first frame and moves the camera without animation
- [x] T-8.9.2.4 The channel page's concept map (moved from T-8.7.3.2): a **Concept map** panel in the Intelligence tab, scoped to the channel (topics and questions), loaded only when opened, plus "Open in the library map"
- [x] T-8.9.2.3 Accessibility: the map is one tab stop (`role="application"`, described by the summary and the key help); arrows move to the nearest node that way, neighbours first; Enter opens the card, Escape closes it, Home returns to the start, + / − / 0 zoom, Shift + arrows pan; a focus ring follows the node through zoom and layout; a live region names each node. Nine buttons replace every drag and wheel gesture (WCAG 2.5.7). The table view lists the same nodes (and, on demand, every connection), opens the same card, and is the default under 640 px. The server's one-paragraph summary heads the map
- Tests: `utils/__tests__/graphModel.test.ts` (10: leaf hiding, groups, colour cap, glyphs, empty graphs, table = drawn set, arrow choice, announcement); `tests/graph.spec.ts` (5, desktop Chromium, synthetic fixture: tab stop and keys, table parity and the card's link, reduced motion never animates, the animated layout freezes, 375 px opens on the table) *2026-11-04, PR [#393](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/393): the animated-layout test polls the observer's record until it holds `running` and `settled`, instead of waiting on `data-layout`, which can match the mount value before the effect runs. That race failed the device runs on the batch 2 and batch 3 landings. With `setLayout('running')` delayed 400 ms the old test failed 3 of 3 and the new one passed 3 of 3; 25 of 25 passed on repeat.*
- Measured (dev server, from navigation start, app load included): 300 nodes, the page's request, first draw 1.3 s; 2,000 nodes 2.2 s animated and 3.0 s under reduced motion, where the layout is computed before the first frame. The scratch library (52 nodes) checked live in both themes
- Found on the way: sigma 4 measures node sizes in graph units by default (`itemSizesReference: "positions"`), which drew every node tens of pixels wide; and its reducers only run on a full `refresh()`

#### S-8.9.3 🔴 Topic clusters
*Parked 2026-10-25 ([D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25)).*
- [ ] T-8.9.3.1 Cluster documents by embedding centroid (k-means over stored chunk means) and colour the graph by cluster; only if S-8.9.2's topic edges prove too sparse
- Scope-changed 2026-10-19: also carries M7's channel topic clusters (the same k-means, scoped to one channel), which S-8.7.2 did not build. The gate needs the library map tried on a real library; S-8.9.2 colours only the three largest Louvain groups (D-092), so clusters would need their own encoding, not a fourth hue

### E-8.10 🟢 Onboarding wizard and empty states (M10)

**Scope.** A first-run flow: pick channels → start a first job from a starter template → watch it run → open the report in the reader; dismissible, resumable, recorded server-side so it survives devices. Plus an audit of every empty state against the editorial voice and a first-run checklist. High leverage for SaaS conversion later, useful for self-hosters now.
**Plan:** [`docs/plans/m10-onboarding.md`](plans/m10-onboarding.md)
**Acceptance.** A new account lands on `/welcome`, completes three steps in under five minutes to a running job, can skip at any point and reopen from the sidebar; `users.onboarded_at` is set on completion; activation events are logged; every list page has an empty state with one primary action.

The offer is made, and spent, only when the Channels form is on screen, never over a draft (any setting changed, on any tab), a cloned run or a template, and once per browser (localStorage). Scope-changed 2026-10-19: (1) step 1 takes channel links or handles instead of "existing search + subscribe": the channels list is per tenant (D-065), so a new account has nothing to pick from, and subscribe starts a full-channel ingest, the opposite of a small first run. (2) The plan's risk note said a channel job does not pause for approval; it does (`job_tasks` channel fetch ends at `awaiting_approval`), so step 3 approves inline. (3) Milestones are derived on read from `jobs` and `audit_log`, so `tasks/job_tasks.py` gained no onboarding code. (4) Plan open questions, taken at the recommended defaults: Q1 a channel job of five videos; Q2 accounts that already have runs are not sent to the wizard, while new and mid-wizard accounts are. (5) The sidebar link shows until the wizard is finished or skipped; after a skip the palette's **Getting started** action reopens it.

### E-8.11 🔴 Public report sharing (M11)

**Scope.** A signed, expiring, revocable URL for a single report, rendered read-only on a public route through the E-8.8 reader with `noindex` and a sandboxing CSP, with view counts, a manage-shares list and takedown hooks. Tier gate `public_report_sharing` already exists.
**Plan:** [`docs/plans/m11-public-report-sharing.md`](plans/m11-public-report-sharing.md)
**Status.** 🔴 deferred (was ⚪ proposed, not accepted): whether sharing launches before billing and the abuse-prevention items it depends on (T-5.5.6 content policy and takedown) is the user's call and is recorded as the plan's first open question. Design is complete so the decision can be taken on a concrete proposal. **Deferred 2026-10-25** ([D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25)): the static site zip covers sharing meanwhile (S-6.2.1 accepts `job:<id>`). If sharing is ever built, the takedown and revoke hooks go inside S-8.11.2, which breaks the circular dependency with T-5.5.6.
**Acceptance.** The owner creates a share with an expiry, copies a URL, and a logged-out visitor reads the report in the reader with no app chrome, no Q&A, no links into the app; the owner revokes it and the next visit returns 404; search engines are told not to index; the public route is rate-limited per IP; takedowns are logged.

#### S-8.11.1 🔴 Share tokens and public route
- [ ] T-8.11.1.1 `report_shares(id, tenant_id, job_id, token_hash, expires_at, revoked_at, view_count, created_at)`; opaque `secrets.token_urlsafe(32)` tokens (256 bits) stored as SHA-256, never JWTs (the app's JWT library carries an unfixed critical CVE, see T-5.4.9); default expiry 30 days with an explicit "no expiry" choice; per-tenant cap on active shares; `SHARING_ENABLED` kill switch
- [ ] T-8.11.1.2 `POST /api/v1/jobs/{id}/share`, `GET /api/v1/jobs/{id}/shares`, `DELETE /api/v1/shares/{id}`; public `GET /s/{token}` JSON for the reader and a raw-HTML variant under `Content-Security-Policy: sandbox; default-src 'none'` (header, not meta); `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow` plus the meta tag (and the path must stay crawlable or the noindex is never seen), `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; per-IP rate limit; a separate origin for shares is the plan's recommendation for SaaS
- [ ] T-8.11.1.3 Public reader route in the frontend (`/s/:token`) using `ReaderShell` with chrome stripped
- [ ] T-8.11.1.4 Tests: expiry, revocation, hashed storage, headers, no leakage of job metadata beyond the report

#### S-8.11.2 🔴 Share UI and takedown hooks
- [ ] T-8.11.2.1 "Share" on the reader and job page; manage list with view counts and revoke
- [ ] T-8.11.2.2 `audit_log` events `share_created` / `share_revoked` / `takedown`; an operator endpoint to revoke by token (T-5.5.6). *If built, this task carries T-5.5.6's takedown and revoke hooks (D-112).*

### E-8.12 🟡 Mobile reading mode (M12)

**Scope-changed 2026-10-25:** 🟢 → 🟡. A real-phone pass is part of M12's acceptance ([D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25)), so the epic is open until S-8.12.3 is done.

**Shipped 2026-10-19** — PRs [#269](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/269) (S-8.12.1) and [#272](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/272) (S-8.12.2), emulation only; the real-device pass is S-8.12.3, proposed and the user's call. Linked decision: [D-087](decisions.md#d-087--mobile-reading-and-install-a-reading-toolbar-below-1200-px-a-docked-composer-lifted-by-visualviewport-hermetic-device-tests-a-manifest-without-a-service-worker-2026-10-19).

**Scope.** Reading-first responsive refinements for reports, Q&A answers and knowledge artifacts: type scale, touch targets, safe areas, dynamic viewport height, a bottom-docked Q&A composer, and device-emulated screenshot tests. Builds on the E-8.8 reader; not a general mobile redesign.
**Plan:** [`docs/plans/m12-mobile-reading.md`](plans/m12-mobile-reading.md)
**Acceptance.** On a 375px-wide viewport the reader, the job Q&A thread and a knowledge artifact are readable without horizontal scroll, every control meets the 44px target, the composer stays reachable while the keyboard is open, and Playwright device-emulation screenshots are checked in for the three pages.

#### S-8.12.1 🟢 Reading pages responsive pass
**PR:** [#269](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/269) — shipped 2026-10-19, emulation only. **Not done: a pass on a real iPhone and a real Android phone.** The plan's risks section calls that part of "done", and its open question 1 asks whether it is part of acceptance; that is the user's call. Emulation has no real keyboard and no Safari chrome, so the VisualViewport lift in particular is unverified on a device.
- [x] T-8.12.1.1 Fluid type scale and measure tokens in `theme.ts`; reader drops the outline under 960px and keeps drop cap and citations (per `ui-design.md` §2.4) — *`fluidType`, `readerScale`, `target` tokens. The outline already hid under 1200 px (S-8.8.1, because the sidebar takes 240 px); below that a bottom reading toolbar with a Contents sheet, text size and theme replaces it, so phones and small tablets keep a way to navigate*
- [x] T-8.12.1.2 Touch targets ≥ 24px everywhere (WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8) and ≥ 44px on primary controls (Apple HIG; inline citation chips are exempt); `env(safe-area-inset-*)` with `viewport-fit=cover`; `100dvh` / `svh` for docked panels; inputs at ≥ 16px to stop iOS zoom and never `maximum-scale=1` (ignored by iOS and a WCAG 1.4.4 failure); 320px reflow (WCAG 1.4.10) — *the toolbar, sheet links, reader actions, hamburger, drawer items, composer field and Ask are 44 px. On touch, a chip's first tap previews its card. Phone page padding follows ui-design §2.2. `dvh` is used for the outline column and the sheet*
- [x] T-8.12.1.3 Bottom-docked Q&A composer on `JobDetailPage` and `LibraryQAPage` under 640px, pinned above the keyboard with the VisualViewport API (rAF-throttled) — *`components/qa/Composer.tsx`: `QADock` / `QAThread` / `DockedComposer` and `useKeyboardInset`, which ignores pinch-zoom. The composer moves under the thread (the API returns it oldest first) and sticks to the bottom while the card is on screen. Nine lines change in each page*
- [x] T-8.12.1.4 Playwright device-emulation screenshot tests (iPhone and Pixel profiles) for the three reading pages — *`npm run test:mobile`: iPhone 13 (WebKit), Pixel 7 and a 320 px phone (Chromium), each in light and dark, giving 18 baselines in `frontend/tests/__screenshots__`. Hermetic: API fixtures with a synthetic Q&A thread, and the WebSocket and proxy are cut off. The tests also assert no horizontal scroll, 44 px controls, the composer in view after a shorter viewport, and 16 px fields*

#### S-8.12.2 🟢 Web-app manifest
**Linked decision:** [D-087](decisions.md#d-087--mobile-reading-and-install-a-reading-toolbar-below-1200-px-a-docked-composer-lifted-by-visualviewport-hermetic-device-tests-a-manifest-without-a-service-worker-2026-10-19)
**PR:** [#272](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/272) — shipped 2026-10-19. Unblocks T-3.7.6.1 (`share_target`).
- [x] T-8.12.2.1 `manifest.webmanifest` (`display: standalone`, icons, `theme_color`; about 30 lines; Safari 26 opens any Home-Screen site as a web app with or without one) and an icon that depends on the logo work in the rebrand table; a service worker is deferred because it complicates JWT-gated fetches and report invalidation and offline reading is not a stated requirement — *the plan left the icon open, so it renders the mark `branding.md` §3 already recommends (three concentric arcs fading inward, oxblood on paper) and uses the §8 inventory's `theme_color: #7a2c1a` and page title. That is an interim rendering of a documented recommendation, not a new logo: the rebrand's logo work stays open, and `npm run icons` re-renders every PNG from `public/logo-mark.svg` when it lands. Ships `logo-mark.svg`, a light / dark `favicon.svg` (replacing Vite's bolt), 192 / 512 / maskable-512 PNGs and a 180 px `apple-touch-icon.png`. Tests: the icons' real sizes and opaque backgrounds, the page's links, and Chromium's own installability check over CDP (no errors, no service worker needed). No service worker*

#### S-8.13.1 🟢 Clarify answers reach the agent
**Shipped:** 2026-10-25 — PR [#376](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/376).
**Found.** The library Q&A page runs a clarify step, then sends the interpretation and the reader's answers as `context` on `POST /library/qa`. `LibraryQARequest` has no `context` field and `run_library_qa_agent` takes none, so they are dropped: "Confirm & search" answers exactly like "Skip clarifications". The shelf panel asks directly for this reason.
- [x] T-8.13.1.1 Check how job Q&A carries clarify context (`schemas/qa.py`, `routers/qa.py`, `run_qa_agent`); if it drops it too, fix both — *job Q&A did not drop it: it already folded `context` into the question. It now uses the shared helper below, so whitespace-only context is no longer appended*
- [x] T-8.13.1.2 `context` (bounded) on `LibraryQARequest`, through `answer_library_question` (library and shelf) into `run_library_qa_agent` and its answer prompt, keeping the shared prompt contracts — *`context` is optional, at most 4,000 characters like job Q&A's. `answer_library_question` folds it into the question with a new shared helper, `qa_prompts.with_clarification`, used by job Q&A too, so it reaches sub-query expansion, the refine prompt and the answer prompt with no template changed. The stored exchange keeps the typed question. Client side, one helper (`utils/clarifyContext.ts`) builds the context for the job and library pages: the reader's answers keep their room, the interpretation is shortened to what is left, and each answer input is capped at 1,000 characters, so "Confirm & search" can no longer turn into a 422*
- [x] T-8.13.1.3 Tests: the router passes `context` to the agent; the agent's answer prompt contains it — *router tests at the job, library and shelf callers (mutation-checked), an agent test that the answer reaches every prompt, a 4,001-character context refused with 422, and vitests pinning what the page sends on Confirm and on Skip*

#### S-8.13.2 🟢 A clarify step on the shelf panel
**Shipped:** 2026-11-04 — PR [#406](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/406) (batch 5 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-132](decisions.md#d-132--qa-brief-keeps-the-whole-thinking-reserve-a-cut-off-report-offers-deep-a-citation-with-no-document-casts-no-vote-and-clarify-is-one-optional-route-2026-11-04). No new route: `POST /library/qa/clarify` sends only the question, so there is no shelf data to scope, and metering matches the shelf ask. BYOK did not match, so the route now opens `byok_context`, reads replies with `response_text` and keeps the thinking reserve on top of its 400 tokens. The library page's step moved verbatim into `components/qa/ClarifyStep.tsx`, used by both surfaces, and its buttons now wrap at 320 px. On the shelf a failed clarify becomes a direct ask with no context, and the panel is keyed on `shelf.id`, so a step does not carry over to the next shelf.
**Filed 2026-10-25** by S-8.13.1 (PR [#376](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/376)); proposed, not yet agreed. `ShelfQAPanel` asks directly, because the context used to be dropped. `POST /shelves/{id}/qa` now accepts and uses `context`, so the panel could run the same clarify step as the library page. A UI change only.

#### S-8.13.3 🟢 The library page stops at a failed clarify
**Shipped:** 2026-11-05 — PR [#419](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/419) (batch 6 of 2026-11-04). Linked decision: [D-145](decisions.md#d-145--a-cut-off-flag-is-a-fact-about-one-reply-the-offer-keeps-the-cards-scope-and-the-server-never-applies-the-accounts-default-depth-2026-11-05). A failed clarify on `LibraryQAPage` becomes a direct ask with no context, as on the shelf panel. The page shows that ask running and takes no second question meanwhile, and what a direct ask carries is held while clarify runs: the depth and language selects, the depth note's Clear and the source chip's way out. The shelf's language select had the same stale read and is held too.
**Filed 2026-11-04** from PR [#406](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/406); proposed, not yet agreed. `LibraryQAPage.handleAsk` has no fall-through, so an outage blocks the ask, as it did before #406. Only the shelf panel changed, because only the shelf ask lost something there. "Skip clarifications" appears only after clarify succeeds, so a failure is a dead end.

## Open questions parking lot

These are real questions raised in conversation that don't yet have a Story home. When one of them is answered the answer becomes a Decision (`D-NNN`) and the question is converted into one or more Stories.

**Planning questions filed 2026-10-19 (the user's call; each lives in its plan, listed here so they are findable):** public sharing before billing and takedown tooling ([m11](plans/m11-public-report-sharing.md)); self-host TTS default and whether clip-stitched B-roll ever exists ([l2](plans/l2-author-studio.md)); private-by-key versus nothing for SaaS Echo, third-party-content default, the bubble shortcut, whether Gmail or Mode B are pursued at all ([l3](plans/l3-echo-personal-brain.md)); per-tier caps on saved searches ([m3](plans/m3-saved-searches-alerts.md)); the note weight's starting value ([m5](plans/m5-notes.md)); a relative publish window in templates ([m6](plans/m6-job-templates.md)); stance classification for YouTube documents ([m7](plans/m7-channel-intelligence.md)); graph library and whether a global view ships ([m9](plans/m9-citation-graph.md)); channel or topic job for the first run ([m10](plans/m10-onboarding.md)); the scoped `<style>` block for the palette ([m2](plans/m2-command-palette.md)).

**Answered 2026-10-25** ([D-110](decisions.md#d-110--echo-only-sees-what-the-user-hands-it-no-background-capture-and-the-echo-scope-rulings-2026-10-25), [D-112](decisions.md#d-112--the-remaining-rulings-of-2026-10-25-map-batching-public-sharing-the-code-mixing-threshold-and-the-quick-calls-2026-10-25)): public sharing (deferred), the TTS default and B-roll, private-by-key (waits until SaaS), the third-party-content default, Gmail, Mode B, the saved-search caps, the relative publish window, stance for YouTube, and the graph questions (D-092 ratified). Not covered by those rulings: the bubble shortcut, the note weight's starting value, the first run's job type and the palette's `<style>` block.

- **OQ-1.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-26 by [D-021](decisions.md#d-021--topic-relevance-threshold--050-2026-04-26): `TOPIC_RELEVANCE_THRESHOLD = 0.50`.** Candidates below 0.50 hidden from default approval list but kept in the database; "Show low-relevance candidates" filter chip toggles cutoff to 0.0. Calibrated scoring (1.0 unambiguous / 0.5 adjacent / 0.0 unrelated) baked into the prompt with borderline 0.4–0.6 exemplars. Re-evaluation hooks documented (precision low → bump 0.60; recall low → drop 0.40; per-source-type override is the next escalation).
- **OQ-2.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-05-06 by [D-046](decisions.md#d-046--comment-tree-depth-per-platform-env-knob-no-per-job-override-resolves-oq-2-2026-05-06): per-platform env knob, no per-job override.** `app/config.py` exposes `REDDIT_COMMENT_DEPTH_DEFAULT` and `HN_COMMENT_DEPTH_DEFAULT` as independent settings (both default 50). No `jobs` schema column, no UI surface, no API parameter. Future platforms follow the same pattern (`<PLATFORM>_COMMENT_DEPTH_DEFAULT`). Per-job override revisits if real demand surfaces; per-platform tuning is a one-line env-var change.
- **OQ-3.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-05-06 by [D-047](decisions.md#d-047--sibling-pr-coordination-for-knowledge-curator--work-tracker-keep-separate-prs-resolves-oq-3-2026-05-06): keep separate PRs as the default.** Each skill owns its own branch + PR; the second-to-fire mentions the sibling via "Companion PR: #N" (already in SKILL.md). Combining is formally **descoped** — the v2 enhancement language in SKILL.md is no longer scheduled. Decoupling avoids one PR blocking the other and keeps each independently reviewable.
- **OQ-4.** Whisper for podcast Mode A vs external service (Deepgram / AssemblyAI) for SaaS tier? (Tied to E-1.7)
- **OQ-5.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-05-06 by [D-048](decisions.md#d-048--pdf-connector-intake-file-upload-only-for-v1-resolves-oq-5-2026-05-06): file upload only for v1.** Ratifies E-1.8 shipped scope (PR [#142](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/142)). URL-fetch deferred to a future Story under E-1.8; identity model (D-034 first-64KB SHA-256) is bytes-derived so URL-fetch lands without schema change. SSRF-protection model from E-1.6 trafilatura URL fetching can be reused when the URL-fetch Story ships.
- **OQ-6.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-05-06 by [D-049](decisions.md#d-049--echo-cold-start-readiness-threshold-shipped-triple-is-v1-personal-brainmd-sub-table-is-forward-looking-spec-resolves-oq-6-2026-05-06): shipped triple is v1.** Three criteria: `total_threshold=100` rows, `sources_threshold=3` distinct sources, `has_personality_trait=True`. The personal-brain.md four-row sub-table (Domains 1/2/3/5 minima) remains the forward-looking spec for once Domain 2 (activity stream) connectors land. Re-evaluation hook: when E-3.4 (speak-as-me agent) consumes `is_ready()` as a gate, mis-calibration becomes load-bearing — validate against real users at that point.
- **OQ-7.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-26 by [D-017](decisions.md#d-017--e-110-hard-cutover-single-migration-uuid-pk-promotion-2026-04-26): hard cutover.** Single Alembic migration adds `document_id UUID` + `source_id text`, backfills both, drops legacy `video_id` PK, adds `(source_type, source_id)` unique constraint, retargets `job_videos` + `transcript_cache` FKs in one transaction. T-1.10.8 (round-trip migration test + 168-test suite + e2e smoke) is gating. Pre-cutover backup `cp data/videoresearchpro.db data/videoresearchpro.db.pre-e110.bak` documented as a self-host fallback.
- **OQ-8.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-26 by [D-018](decisions.md#d-018--polymorphic-approvalcard-typescript-shape--four-sub-decisions-2026-04-26).** Locked-in shape lives in [source-types.md § Polymorphic ApprovalCard TypeScript shape](source-types.md#polymorphic-approvalcard-typescript-shape). User's overarching framing: *"keep the system a bit open ended for future enhancements and not too strict"*.
  - **(a)** ` SourceMetadata` hand-rolled in TS, kept synced with backend Pydantic by convention. Drift is a PR-review concern. Revisit if drift count climbs.
  - **(b)** Chip ` field` is ` keyof T` — pure source-metadata. Document-level fields render through fixed slots in ` <CardHeader>` and ` <CardActions>`, not chips.
  - **(c)** Hybrid formatters — named registry (` durationSeconds`, ` relativeTime`, ` signedNumber`, ` numberWithCommas`, ` truncate`) + optional ` format?: (v) => string` callback override.
  - **(d)** Separate ` FilterChip<T>` type. Source configs register two distinct arrays: ` metaChips` (display) and ` filterChips` (predicate).
  - T-1.5.4.1 unblocked. PR-review-driven drift correction documented as a revisit hook on (a); promotion to flat ` View<T>` documented as a revisit hook on (b) if a future source type wants Document-level chips.
- **OQ-9.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-26 by [D-019](decisions.md#d-019--codeowners--branch-protection-policy-for-autonomous-merge-sessions-2026-04-26).** User picked option (c) bypass list, but `bypass_pull_request_allowances` is not exposed on personal-account free-plan public repos (verified via PATCH that silently dropped the field). Pragmatic landing: `.github/CODEOWNERS` declares `@khoks` as owner of every path, and `required_approving_review_count` dropped to `0`. Net: `gh pr merge --squash --delete-branch` (no `--admin`) works on master immediately. Force-push still blocked. Two revisit hooks documented (second collaborator joins → Rulesets `bypass_actors`; org migration → `bypass_pull_request_allowances.users`).
- **OQ-10.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-26 by [D-020](decisions.md#d-020--file-orchestrator-dispatch-as-standalone-story-s-1511-2026-04-26): file as standalone S-1.5.11.** Dispatch layer ships once with the first two consumers (Reddit + HN) and is reused by every future connector. Folding into per-source storage tasks would duplicate the dispatch pattern N times. See [S-1.5.11](#s-1511--topic-job-routing-through-new-connectors) for the task breakdown.
- **OQ-12.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-28 by [D-023](decisions.md#d-023--social_classify_stance-invoked-inline-inside-each-connector-2026-04-28): option (a) inline.** Each `BaseConnector` subclass calls `social_classify` from `app/services/social_classify.py` on its fetched text before returning Candidates. T-1.5.3.3 acceptance updated to "Inline call inside `RedditConnector.fetch_text()` and `HNConnector.fetch_text()` (and future connectors)."
- **OQ-13.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-28 — ship now as a tiny PR.** User approved. Post-OQ-11 dev wipe collapsed the rename from a high-risk migration to a one-line env default change. T-2.6.1 ships in this work cycle; T-2.6.6 documents the production-data migration path separately for self-hosters with accumulated embeddings.
- **OQ-14.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-28 by [D-024](decisions.md#d-024--flip-e-16-to--with-primitives-only-scope-split-2026-04-28): option (b) primitives-only split.** E-1.6 flips 🔴 → 🔵. trafilatura + Playwright primitives ship now as `app/services/article_extraction/` in service of S-1.5.8 Mode B paste. Full article-connector UX (RSS / search / approval card variant) stays deferred until after M-1.5. D-005 amended.
- **OQ-15.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-28 by [D-025](decisions.md#d-025--file-mvp-definition-of-done-as-milestone-m-15-2026-04-28).** Milestone M-1.5 — Reddit + HN end-to-end ingest filed under E-1.5 with 7 component checks: (1) E-1.10 cutover landed; (2) S-1.5.11 dispatcher; (3) T-1.5.1.4 + T-1.5.2.5 storage; (4) S-1.5.3 inline classifier per D-023; (5) S-1.5.4 polymorphic ApprovalCard with badges + filters; (6) S-1.5.5 citation rendering; (7) e2e pipeline tests for Reddit-only / HN-only / mixed source types.
- **OQ-11.** ✅ **Resolved 2026-04-28 — option (b) cleanest-reset chosen.** User authorized full data wipe ("I don't care about existing data and jobs"). Executed in this session:
  - Moved `data/videoresearchpro.db` (3.4 MB, 912 legacy `videos` rows + the empty post-rename `documents` table + all jobs / channels / Q&A history / transcript cache) to `data/.pre-cleanup-2026-04-28/`.
  - Moved `data/chroma/` (22 MB embedding store) to `data/.pre-cleanup-2026-04-28/` so dangling chunk metadata referencing wiped video_ids doesn't surface in Q&A retrieval.
  - Removed leftover test DBs (`data/test-e110-t1101.db`, `data/test-trace.db`).
  - Ran `alembic upgrade head` against an empty DB. **Full migration chain runs cleanly end-to-end**, including PR [#96](https://github.com/khoks/VideoResearchPro/pull/96)'s merge-node + my T-1.10.1 `document_id` UUID column.
  - Final state verified: `alembic_version = d12345678901` (single head, no parallel branches), all 11 tables present (`alembic_version`, `api_quota_log`, `channels`, `documents`, `job_videos`, `jobs`, `library_qa_exchanges`, `qa_exchanges`, `qa_history_exchanges`, `transcript_cache`, `users`), `documents.document_id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL` exists with `ix_documents_document_id` unique index. FastAPI app imports cleanly (42 routes registered, title `Pratidhvani`).
  - The pre-cleanup snapshot is preserved in `data/.pre-cleanup-2026-04-28/` for paranoia; can be deleted whenever.
  - Side discovery: two pre-existing `test_llm_routing.py` tests were failing on master since `LLM_PRIMARY_MODEL` was added to `app/config.py` — they monkeypatch only `LLM_MODEL` but `_resolve_primary_model()` prefers `LLM_PRIMARY_MODEL`. Drive-by fixed alongside this cleanup.
- S-1.11.10 🟢 IP-block workarounds (D-051 hooks exercised 2026-07-22): `YOUTUBE_PROXY_URL` routes both the transcript API (GenericProxyConfig) and yt-dlp through an operator-supplied proxy; `YTDLP_COOKIES_FROM_BROWSER` / `YTDLP_COOKIES_FILE` (explicit opt-in) pass YouTube's "confirm you're not a bot" wall using the operator's own session; `YTDLP_DOWNLOAD_RATE_LIMIT` paces download attempts (the 60-video recovery sweep's ~180 burst downloads escalated the block). Diagnosis that motivated this: all 32 residual unavailable videos are public+live, failing only on fetch (transcript API `RequestBlocked` + yt-dlp bot-wall on all three player clients).
